```
# --- cluster model ---------------------------------------------------------------


def junction_chunks(c):
    """First and last chunk of the junction segment of one box centered at c."""
    return odd_below(c - HALF - 1) // CHUNK, odd_above(c + HALF + 1) // CHUNK


class ClusterScene:
    """Boxes on one shaft whose junction segments share a chunk. One junction segment spans them all."""

    def __init__(self, seed, centers, sampler, steps_per_node, chunks_each_side=3):
        self.centers = sorted(centers)
        rng = random.Random(f"{seed}:cluster:{self.centers}")
        self.signs = [rng.choice((1, -1)) for _ in self.centers]
        self.k = min(junction_chunks(c)[0] for c in self.centers)
        self.k_end = max(junction_chunks(c)[1] for c in self.centers)
        self.c_lo = (self.k - chunks_each_side) // 2 * 2
        self.c_hi = (self.k_end + chunks_each_side + 1) // 2 * 2
        self.segments = {}
        for c in range(self.c_lo, self.c_hi + 1):
            if self.k <= c <= self.k_end:
                continue
            self.segments[c] = build_segment(segment_kind("connector", c), NODES_PER_CHUNK, seg_rng(seed, "connector", c), sampler, steps_per_node)
        self.shaft_edges = Scene._plain_shaft_edges(self)
        self.shaft_edges += self._junction_segment(seg_rng(seed, "junction", self.k), sampler, steps_per_node)

    def rulings(self):
        start = CHUNK * self.k + 1
        end = CHUNK * (self.k_end + 1)
        return [x for x in range(start, end, 2) if all(abs(x - c) > HALF for c in self.centers)]

    def _junction_segment(self, rng, sampler, steps_per_node):
        g = Graph()
        names = {}
        index = {}
        rulings = self.rulings()
        for x in rulings:
            for v in range(RING):
                n = g.add_node()
                index[(x, v)] = n
                names[n] = (x, v)
        for x in rulings:
            for v in range(RING):
                g.add_edge(index[(x, v)], index[(x, (v + 1) % RING)])
                if (x + 2, v) in index and not is_corner(v):
                    g.add_edge(index[(x, v)], index[(x + 2, v)])
        for b, (c, sign) in enumerate(zip(self.centers, self.signs)):
            patch = patch_grid(g)
            for (i, j), n in patch.items():
                names[n] = ("patch", b, i, j)
            x_last, x_first = odd_below(c - HALF - 1), odd_above(c + HALF + 1)
            for j in range(-2, 3):
                v = face_node(sign, j)
                g.add_edge(patch[(-3, j)], index[(x_last, v)])
                g.add_edge(patch[(3, j)], index[(x_first, v)])
        x_start, x_end = rulings[0], rulings[-1]
        if self.k % 2 == 1:
            left = g.add_node()
            names[left] = (x_start - 2, None)
            for v in range(RING):
                if not is_corner(v):
                    g.add_edge(left, index[(x_start, v)])
        if self.k_end % 2 == 1:
            right = g.add_node()
            names[right] = (x_end + 2, None)
            for v in range(RING):
                if not is_corner(v):
                    g.add_edge(right, index[(x_end, v)])
        parent = sample(g, rng, sampler, steps_per_node)
        edges = []
        for a, b in tree_edges(g, parent, names):
            if a[1] is None:
                a = (a[0], b[1])
            if b[1] is None:
                b = (b[0], a[1])
            edges.append((a, b))
        return prune_corner_leaves(edges, lambda n: n[0] != "patch" and is_corner(n[1]))


def cluster_stats(seed, count, sampler, spn):
    """Place two boxes at a random distance of 31 to 60 blocks and check that the shaft stays one tree."""
    merged = 0
    trees = 0
    for k in range(count):
        rng = random.Random(f"{seed}:cluster-place:{k}")
        c1 = rng.randrange(100, 400)
        c2 = c1 + rng.randrange(31, 61)
        if junction_chunks(c1)[1] >= junction_chunks(c2)[0]:
            merged += 1
            scene = ClusterScene(seed, [c1, c2], sampler, spn)
```

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze;

/**
 * The maze grid around one shaft, seen as a ring of 48 blocks.
 * Block 0 is the corner at offset (-6, 6). The ring runs along the face where the second offset is 6.
 * Nodes sit on even blocks, so node {@code v} is block {@code 2v}.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
public final class Ring {

    public static final int BLOCKS = 48;
    public static final int NODES = BLOCKS / 2;
    public static final int FACE_NODES = 6;
    /** The offset of the outer layer from the shaft center. */
    public static final int OUTER = 6;
    /** The offset of the junction box face from the junction center. */
    public static final int BOX = 7;

    private Ring() {
    }

    public static boolean isCorner(int node) {
        return node % FACE_NODES == 0;
    }

    public static int first(int block) {
        block = Math.floorMod(block, BLOCKS);
        if (block < 12) {
            return -OUTER + block;
        }
        if (block < 24) {
            return OUTER;
        }
        if (block < 36) {
            return OUTER - (block - 24);
        }
        return -OUTER;
    }

    public static int second(int block) {
        block = Math.floorMod(block, BLOCKS);
        if (block < 12) {
            return OUTER;
        }
        if (block < 24) {
            return OUTER - (block - 12);
        }
        if (block < 36) {
            return -OUTER;
        }
        return -OUTER + (block - 36);
    }

    /** The ring block at the offset, or -1 when the offset is not on the outer layer. */
    public static int block(int first, int second) {
        if (second == OUTER && first >= -OUTER && first < OUTER) {
            return first + OUTER;
        }
        if (first == OUTER && second > -OUTER && second <= OUTER) {
            return 12 + (OUTER - second);
        }
        if (second == -OUTER && first > -OUTER && first <= OUTER) {
            return 24 + (OUTER - first);
        }
        if (first == -OUTER && second >= -OUTER && second < OUTER) {
            return 36 + (second + OUTER);
        }
        return -1;
    }

    /** The node on the face where the second offset is {@code 6 * sign}, at the first offset {@code 2k}. */
    public static int faceNode(int sign, int k) {
        return block(2 * k, OUTER * sign) / 2;
    }

    /** The ring block between two neighbor nodes. */
    public static int blockBetween(int node1, int node2) {
        return Math.abs(node1 - node2) == 1 ? 2 * Math.min(node1, node2) + 1 : BLOCKS - 1;
    }

    public static int oddBelow(int a) {
        return (a & 1) != 0 ? a : a - 1;
    }

    public static int oddAbove(int a) {
        return (a & 1) != 0 ? a : a + 1;
    }

}
```

</details>

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze;

import it.unimi.dsi.fastutil.ints.IntArrayList;
import it.unimi.dsi.fastutil.ints.IntList;
import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * An undirected graph whose adjacency lists keep the insertion sequence, with the Origin Shift sampler.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
public final class MazeGraph {

    /** The Origin Shift steps per node. The stats mode of the maze prototype matches Wilson's algorithm at this count. */
    public static final int STEPS_PER_NODE = 16;

    private final List<IntList> adjacency = new ArrayList<>();

    public int addNode() {
        this.adjacency.add(new IntArrayList(4));
        return this.adjacency.size() - 1;
    }

    public void addEdge(int a, int b) {
        this.adjacency.get(a).add(b);
        this.adjacency.get(b).add(a);
    }

    public int size() {
        return this.adjacency.size();
    }

    /**
     * Samples a spanning tree with Origin Shift from the breadth-first tree of a random root.
     *
     * @return the parent of each node, -1 for the root
     */
    public int[] sampleTree(RandomSource random) {
        int root = random.nextInt(this.size());
        int[] parent = this.breadthFirstTree(root);
        long steps = (long) STEPS_PER_NODE * this.size();
        for (long step = 0; step < steps; step++) {
            IntList neighbors = this.adjacency.get(root);
            int next = neighbors.getInt(random.nextInt(neighbors.size()));
            parent[root] = next;
            root = next;
            parent[root] = -1;
        }
        return parent;
    }

    private int[] breadthFirstTree(int root) {
        int[] parent = new int[this.size()];
        java.util.Arrays.fill(parent, -2);
        parent[root] = -1;
        int[] queue = new int[this.size()];
        int head = 0;
        int tail = 0;
        queue[tail++] = root;
        while (head < tail) {
            int node = queue[head++];
            IntList neighbors = this.adjacency.get(node);
            for (int k = 0; k < neighbors.size(); k++) {
                int next = neighbors.getInt(k);
                if (parent[next] == -2) {
                    parent[next] = node;
                    queue[tail++] = next;
                }
            }
        }
        return parent;
    }

}
```

```
/**
```

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.function.IntFunction;

/**
 * A tree edge of the maze, which carves a maze path.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
public record MazeEdge(MazeNode a, MazeNode b) {

    /** Collects the tree edges of a sampled graph. A node without a name is a virtual node. */
    static List<MazeEdge> treeEdges(int[] parent, IntFunction<MazeNode> names, IntFunction<MazeNode> virtualTargets) {
        List<MazeEdge> edges = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int node = 0; node < parent.length; node++) {
            int p = parent[node];
            if (p < 0) {
                continue;
            }
            MazeNode a = names.apply(node);
            MazeNode b = names.apply(p);
            if (a == null) {
                a = virtualTargets.apply(node);
            }
            if (b == null) {
                b = virtualTargets.apply(p);
            }
            edges.add(new MazeEdge(a, b));
        }
        return edges;
    }

    /** Drops every corner node of degree one together with its edge. */
    static List<MazeEdge> pruneCornerLeaves(List<MazeEdge> edges) {
        Map<MazeNode, Integer> degree = new HashMap<>();
        for (MazeEdge edge : edges) {
            degree.merge(edge.a, 1, Integer::sum);
            degree.merge(edge.b, 1, Integer::sum);
        }
        List<MazeEdge> kept = new ArrayList<>(edges.size());
        for (MazeEdge edge : edges) {
            if (!isCornerLeaf(edge.a, degree) && !isCornerLeaf(edge.b, degree)) {
                kept.add(edge);
            }
        }
        return kept;
    }

    private static boolean isCornerLeaf(MazeNode node, Map<MazeNode, Integer> degree) {
        return node instanceof MazeNode.Ruling ruling && ruling.isCorner() && degree.get(node) == 1;
    }

}
```

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze;

import it.unimi.dsi.fastutil.ints.Int2IntMap;
import it.unimi.dsi.fastutil.ints.Int2IntOpenHashMap;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * The nodes of a set of rulings in one graph, with the names of all nodes.
 * Ring edges join neighbor nodes of one ruling. Axis edges join two rulings two blocks apart.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
final class RulingGraph {

    final MazeGraph graph = new MazeGraph();
    private final List<MazeNode> names = new ArrayList<>();
    private final Int2IntMap firstNodeOfRuling = new Int2IntOpenHashMap();

    RulingGraph(int[] rulings) {
        for (int along : rulings) {
            this.firstNodeOfRuling.put(along, this.graph.size());
            for (int v = 0; v < Ring.NODES; v++) {
                this.add(new MazeNode.Ruling(along, v));
            }
        }
        for (int along : rulings) {
            for (int v = 0; v < Ring.NODES; v++) {
                this.graph.addEdge(this.node(along, v), this.node(along, (v + 1) % Ring.NODES));
                if (this.firstNodeOfRuling.containsKey(along + 2) && !Ring.isCorner(v)) {
                    this.graph.addEdge(this.node(along, v), this.node(along + 2, v));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    int node(int along, int v) {
        return this.firstNodeOfRuling.get(along) + v;
    }

    int add(MazeNode name) {
        this.names.add(name);
        return this.graph.addNode();
    }

    int addVirtual() {
        this.names.add(null);
        return this.graph.addNode();
    }

    /** Adds a virtual node that touches every interior node of one ruling. */
    int addVirtual(int along) {
        int virtual = this.addVirtual();
        for (int v = 0; v < Ring.NODES; v++) {
            if (!Ring.isCorner(v)) {
                this.graph.addEdge(virtual, this.node(along, v));
            }
        }
        return virtual;
    }

    /** Adds the 7x7 patch nodes of one junction box and their grid edges. Returns the node of (-3, -3). */
    int addPatch(int owner) {
        int first = this.graph.size();
        for (int i = -3; i <= 3; i++) {
            for (int j = -3; j <= 3; j++) {
                this.add(new MazeNode.Patch(owner, i, j));
            }
        }
        for (int i = -3; i <= 3; i++) {
            for (int j = -3; j <= 3; j++) {
                int n = patchNode(first, i, j);
                if (i < 3) {
                    this.graph.addEdge(n, patchNode(first, i + 1, j));
                }
                if (j < 3) {
                    this.graph.addEdge(n, patchNode(first, i, j + 1));
                }
            }
        }
        return first;
    }

    static int patchNode(int first, int i, int j) {
        return first + (i + 3) * 7 + (j + 3);
    }

    MazeNode[] names() {
        return this.names.toArray(MazeNode[]::new);
    }

}
```

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze;

import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.PositionalRandomFactory;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * The maze of one horizontal shaft, cut into chunk segments.
 * Tree segments sit on even chunks and connector segments on odd chunks.
 * A junction segment replaces the chunks around one or more junction boxes.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
public final class ShaftMaze {

    public static final int CHUNK = 16;
    public static final int RULINGS_PER_CHUNK = CHUNK / 2;

    private static final int PLAIN = 0;
    private static final int JUNCTION = 1;

    private final BoxLookup boxes;
    private final PositionalRandomFactory random;

    public ShaftMaze(BoxLookup boxes, PositionalRandomFactory random) {
        this.boxes = boxes;
        this.random = random;
    }

    /** A junction box on the shaft. The shaft patch is on the face at {@code 7 * sign} along the second offset. */
    public record Box(int center, int sign) {

        public int lastRulingBefore() {
            return Ring.oddBelow(this.center - Ring.BOX - 1);
        }

        public int firstRulingAfter() {
            return Ring.oddAbove(this.center + Ring.BOX + 1);
        }

        public int firstChunk() {
            return Math.floorDiv(this.lastRulingBefore(), CHUNK);
        }

        public int lastChunk() {
            return Math.floorDiv(this.firstRulingAfter(), CHUNK);
        }

        public boolean covers(int along) {
            return Math.abs(along - this.center) <= Ring.BOX;
        }
    }

    /** Finds the boxes of the shaft whose centers lie in a block range. */
    @FunctionalInterface
    public interface BoxLookup {
        List<Box> boxes(int minCenter, int maxCenter);
    }

    /**
     * One segment of the shaft maze.
     *
     * @param rulings the rulings of the segment, in ascending sequence
     * @param edges   the tree edges, with crossings into the neighbor segments
     */
    public record Segment(int firstChunk, int lastChunk, List<Box> boxes, int[] rulings, List<MazeEdge> edges) {
    }

    /** The segment that holds the chunk at index {@code chunk} along the shaft axis. */
    public Segment segmentAt(int chunk) {
        List<Box> members = this.boxesIn(chunk, chunk, true);
        if (members.isEmpty()) {
            return this.plain(chunk);
        }
        while (true) {
            int lo = members.stream().mapToInt(Box::firstChunk).min().orElseThrow();
            int hi = members.stream().mapToInt(Box::lastChunk).max().orElseThrow();
            List<Box> found = this.boxesIn(lo, hi, false);
            if (found.size() == members.size()) {
                return this.junction(lo, hi, found);
            }
            members = found;
        }
    }

    private List<Box> boxesIn(int lo, int hi, boolean containing) {
        List<Box> found = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Box box : this.boxes.boxes(CHUNK * lo - CHUNK, CHUNK * hi + 2 * CHUNK)) {
            boolean hit = containing
                    ? box.firstChunk() <= lo && lo <= box.lastChunk()
                    : box.lastChunk() >= lo && box.firstChunk() <= hi;
            if (hit) {
                found.add(box);
            }
        }
        found.sort(Comparator.comparingInt(Box::center));
        return found;
    }

    private Segment plain(int chunk) {
        int base = CHUNK * chunk;
        int[] rulings = new int[RULINGS_PER_CHUNK];
        for (int u = 0; u < RULINGS_PER_CHUNK; u++) {
            rulings[u] = base + 2 * u + 1;
        }
        RulingGraph graph = new RulingGraph(rulings);
        int left = -1;
        int right = -1;
        if ((chunk & 1) != 0) {
            left = graph.addVirtual(rulings[0]);
            right = graph.addVirtual(rulings[rulings.length - 1]);
        }
```

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze;

import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.PositionalRandomFactory;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * The maze of one column. Its junctions cut it into pieces, and each piece is one tree.
 * Each column patch is one tree with one virtual node per neighbor piece.
 *
 * @param pieces  the pieces from the bottom build limit to the top build limit
 * @param patches the column patch trees, one per junction from the bottom up
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
public record ColumnMaze(List<Piece> pieces, List<PatchTree> patches) {

    private static final int PIECE = 0;
    private static final int PATCH = 1;

    /**
     * A junction on the column.
     *
     * @param center the Y of the junction center
     * @param sign   the sign of the column patch face, along the second offset of the horizontal shaft
     * @param alongX true when the horizontal shaft runs along X
     */
    public record Junction(int center, int sign, boolean alongX) {

        /** The column ring node under the patch node at {@code 2i} along the horizontal shaft. */
        public int columnNode(int i) {
            int block = this.alongX
                    ? Ring.block(2 * i, Ring.OUTER * this.sign)
                    : Ring.block(Ring.OUTER * this.sign, 2 * i);
            return block / 2;
        }
    }

    /** The rulings from {@code first} to {@code last}, every two blocks. The piece is empty when first exceeds last. */
    public record Piece(int first, int last, List<MazeEdge> edges) {

        public boolean isEmpty() {
            return this.first > this.last;
        }
    }

    public record PatchTree(Junction junction, List<MazeEdge> edges) {
    }

    /** The first ruling above a junction, or above the bottom build limit for the first piece. */
    static int pieceFirst(int minY, List<Junction> junctions, int k) {
        return k == 0 ? Ring.oddAbove(minY) : Ring.oddAbove(junctions.get(k - 1).center() + Ring.BOX + 1);
    }

    /** The last ruling below a junction, or below the top build limit for the last piece. */
    static int pieceLast(int maxY, List<Junction> junctions, int k) {
        return k == junctions.size() ? Ring.oddBelow(maxY - 1) : Ring.oddBelow(junctions.get(k).center() - Ring.BOX - 1);
    }

    /** True when every piece between two junctions holds at least one ruling. */
    public static boolean fits(List<Junction> junctions) {
        for (int k = 1; k < junctions.size(); k++) {
            if (pieceFirst(0, junctions, k) > pieceLast(0, junctions, k)) {
                return false;
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

    /**
     * Samples the maze of a column.
     *
     * @param maxY      the top build limit, exclusive
     * @param junctions the junctions in ascending sequence of Y
     */
    public static ColumnMaze build(int minY, int maxY, List<Junction> junctions, PositionalRandomFactory random) {
        List<Piece> pieces = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int k = 0; k <= junctions.size(); k++) {
            pieces.add(piece(pieceFirst(minY, junctions, k), pieceLast(maxY, junctions, k), random.at(PIECE, k, 0)));
        }
        List<PatchTree> patches = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int k = 0; k < junctions.size(); k++) {
            patches.add(patch(junctions.get(k), pieces.get(k), pieces.get(k + 1), random.at(PATCH, k, 0)));
        }
        return new ColumnMaze(List.copyOf(pieces), List.copyOf(patches));
    }

    private static Piece piece(int first, int last, net.minecraft.util.RandomSource random) {
        if (first > last) {
            return new Piece(first, last, List.of());
        }
        int[] rulings = new int[(last - first) / 2 + 1];
        for (int u = 0; u < rulings.length; u++) {
            rulings[u] = first + 2 * u;
        }
```

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.world;

import com.mojang.serialization.Codec;
import com.mojang.serialization.DataResult;
import com.mojang.serialization.codecs.RecordCodecBuilder;
import net.minecraft.util.Mth;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;

/**
 * The settings of the preset. The player sets them in the world creation menu.
 *
 * @param shaftSpacing   the mean distance between horizontal shafts along one axis
 * @param columnChance   the chance that a crossing of two shafts in plan view gets a column
 * @param decayScale     the size of one decay region along a shaft, a power of two
 * @param decayThreshold decay noise below the negative threshold keeps only maze walls, above it only maze paths
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
public record AeroliteSettings(
        int shaftSpacing,
        float columnChance,
        int decayScale,
        float decayThreshold,
        BlockState interiorBlock,
        BlockState wallBlock,
        BlockState pathBlock
) {

    public static final int MIN_SHAFT_SPACING = 32;
    public static final int MAX_SHAFT_SPACING = 4096;
    public static final int MIN_DECAY_SCALE = 32;
    public static final int MAX_DECAY_SCALE = 1024;

    public static final AeroliteSettings DEFAULT = new AeroliteSettings(
            256,
            0.25F,
            256,
            0.5F,
            Blocks.STONE.defaultBlockState(),
            Blocks.STONE_BRICKS.defaultBlockState(),
            Blocks.COBBLESTONE.defaultBlockState()
    );

    private static final Codec<Integer> DECAY_SCALE_CODEC = Codec.intRange(MIN_DECAY_SCALE, MAX_DECAY_SCALE).validate(scale -> Mth.isPowerOfTwo(scale)
            ? DataResult.success(scale)
            : DataResult.error(() -> "Decay scale is not a power of two: " + scale));

    public static final Codec<AeroliteSettings> CODEC = RecordCodecBuilder.create(instance -> instance.group(
            Codec.intRange(MIN_SHAFT_SPACING, MAX_SHAFT_SPACING).optionalFieldOf("shaft_spacing", DEFAULT.shaftSpacing).forGetter(AeroliteSettings::shaftSpacing),
            Codec.floatRange(0, 1).optionalFieldOf("column_chance", DEFAULT.columnChance).forGetter(AeroliteSettings::columnChance),
            DECAY_SCALE_CODEC.optionalFieldOf("decay_scale", DEFAULT.decayScale).forGetter(AeroliteSettings::decayScale),
            Codec.floatRange(0, 1).optionalFieldOf("decay_threshold", DEFAULT.decayThreshold).forGetter(AeroliteSettings::decayThreshold),
            BlockState.CODEC.optionalFieldOf("interior_block", DEFAULT.interiorBlock).forGetter(AeroliteSettings::interiorBlock),
            BlockState.CODEC.optionalFieldOf("wall_block", DEFAULT.wallBlock).forGetter(AeroliteSettings::wallBlock),
            BlockState.CODEC.optionalFieldOf("path_block", DEFAULT.pathBlock).forGetter(AeroliteSettings::pathBlock)
    ).apply(instance, AeroliteSettings::new));

    public AeroliteSettings withDecayScale(int scale) {
        return new AeroliteSettings(this.shaftSpacing, this.columnChance, scale, this.decayThreshold, this.interiorBlock, this.wallBlock, this.pathBlock);
    }

    public AeroliteSettings withDecayThreshold(float threshold) {
        return new AeroliteSettings(this.shaftSpacing, this.columnChance, this.decayScale, threshold, this.interiorBlock, this.wallBlock, this.pathBlock);
    }

}
```

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;

/**
 * Maps shaft coordinates to world coordinates.
 * A shaft coordinate is a block along the shaft axis plus a first and a second offset from the shaft center.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 *
 * @param center1 the world coordinate of the first offset origin
 * @param center2 the world coordinate of the second offset origin
 */
public record ShaftFrame(Kind kind, int center1, int center2) {

    public enum Kind {
        /** Along X. The first offset is Y, the second offset is Z. */
        ALONG_X,
        /** Along Z. The first offset is Y, the second offset is X. */
        ALONG_Z,
        /** Along Y. The first offset is X, the second offset is Z. */
        COLUMN
    }

    public static ShaftFrame of(ShaftLayout.Shaft shaft) {
        return shaft.axis() == ShaftLayout.Axis.X
                ? new ShaftFrame(Kind.ALONG_X, shaft.centerY(), shaft.position())
                : new ShaftFrame(Kind.ALONG_Z, shaft.centerY(), shaft.position());
    }

    public static ShaftFrame of(ShaftLayout.Column column) {
        return new ShaftFrame(Kind.COLUMN, column.x(), column.z());
    }

    public BlockPos at(int along, int first, int second) {
        return switch (this.kind) {
            case ALONG_X -> new BlockPos(along, this.center1 + first, this.center2 + second);
            case ALONG_Z -> new BlockPos(this.center2 + second, this.center1 + first, along);
            case COLUMN -> new BlockPos(this.center1 + first, along, this.center2 + second);
        };
    }

    /** The direction of a positive second offset times {@code sign}. */
    public Direction secondDirection(int sign) {
        return switch (this.kind) {
            case ALONG_X, COLUMN -> sign > 0 ? Direction.SOUTH : Direction.NORTH;
            case ALONG_Z -> sign > 0 ? Direction.EAST : Direction.WEST;
        };
    }

}
```

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft;

import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.synth.SimplexNoise;

/**
 * Fractal Brownian simplex noise in three dimensions, in the range -1 to 1.
 * Each octave doubles the frequency and halves the amplitude.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
public final class FractalNoise {

    private final SimplexNoise[] octaves;
    private final double frequency;
    private final double amplitudeSum;

    /**
     * @param scale the size in blocks of one feature of the first octave
     */
    public FractalNoise(RandomSource random, int octaveCount, double scale) {
        this.octaves = new SimplexNoise[octaveCount];
        double sum = 0;
        for (int k = 0; k < octaveCount; k++) {
            this.octaves[k] = new SimplexNoise(random);
            sum += 1.0 / (1 << k);
        }
        this.frequency = 1.0 / scale;
        this.amplitudeSum = sum;
    }

    public double sample(int x, int y, int z) {
        double value = 0;
        double frequency = this.frequency;
        double amplitude = 1;
        for (SimplexNoise octave : this.octaves) {
            value += amplitude * octave.getValue(x * frequency, y * frequency, z * frequency);
            frequency *= 2;
            amplitude *= 0.5;
        }
        return value / this.amplitudeSum;
    }

}
```

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze.ColumnMaze;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze.MazeEdge;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze.MazeNode;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze.Ring;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze.ShaftMaze;

import javax.annotation.Nullable;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.function.Consumer;
import java.util.function.Function;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * Turns maze nodes and tree edges into maze path blocks.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
final class MazeCarver {

    /**
     * One maze path block.
     *
     * @param surface the maze grid block, which becomes air
     * @param beneath the block under it, which becomes the maze path block
     * @param normal  the outward face direction when the block is on a patch
     */
    record Carve(BlockPos surface, BlockPos beneath, @Nullable Direction normal) {
    }

    /** A patch face: the frame of its horizontal shaft, the face sign and the junction center along that shaft. */
    record PatchFace(ShaftFrame frame, int sign, int center) {

        Carve carve(int along, int first) {
            return new Carve(
                    this.frame.at(along, first, Ring.BOX * this.sign),
                    this.frame.at(along, first, (Ring.BOX - 1) * this.sign),
                    this.frame.secondDirection(this.sign)
            );
        }

        Carve node(int i, int j) {
            return this.carve(this.center + 2 * i, 2 * j);
        }
    }

    private MazeCarver() {
    }

    static Carve block(ShaftFrame frame, int along, int block) {
        int first = Ring.first(block);
        int second = Ring.second(block);
        return new Carve(frame.at(along, first, second), frame.at(along, clampInterior(first), clampInterior(second)), null);
    }

    private static int clampInterior(int offset) {
        return Math.max(-Ring.OUTER + 1, Math.min(Ring.OUTER - 1, offset));
    }

    /** Carves the ruling nodes of the rulings. A corner node carves only when an edge holds it. */
    static void rulings(ShaftFrame frame, int[] rulings, List<MazeEdge> edges, Consumer<Carve> out) {
        Set<MazeNode> present = new HashSet<>();
        for (MazeEdge edge : edges) {
            present.add(edge.a());
            present.add(edge.b());
        }
        for (int along : rulings) {
            for (int v = 0; v < Ring.NODES; v++) {
                if (!Ring.isCorner(v) || present.contains(new MazeNode.Ruling(along, v))) {
                    out.accept(block(frame, along, 2 * v));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    static void rulingEdge(ShaftFrame frame, MazeNode.Ruling a, MazeNode.Ruling b, Consumer<Carve> out) {
        if (a.along() == b.along()) {
            out.accept(block(frame, a.along(), Ring.blockBetween(a.node(), b.node())));
        } else {
            out.accept(block(frame, Math.min(a.along(), b.along()) + 1, 2 * a.node()));
        }
    }

    /** Carves the edges and patch nodes of one horizontal shaft segment. */
    static void shaftSegment(ShaftFrame frame, ShaftMaze.Segment segment, Consumer<Carve> out) {
        Map<Integer, PatchFace> faces = segment.boxes().stream()

        for (PatchFace face : faces.values()) {
            patchNodes(face, out);
        }
        rulings(frame, segment.rulings(), segment.edges(), out);
        edges(frame, segment.edges(), faces::get, out);
    }
```

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft;

import it.unimi.dsi.fastutil.longs.Long2ObjectMap;
import it.unimi.dsi.fastutil.longs.Long2ObjectOpenHashMap;
import it.unimi.dsi.fastutil.longs.LongOpenHashSet;
import it.unimi.dsi.fastutil.longs.LongSet;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import net.minecraft.world.level.ChunkPos;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.ChunkAccess;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.AeroliteSettings;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze.ColumnMaze;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze.Ring;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze.ShaftMaze;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Writes the shafts, columns and junctions of one chunk.
 * Volumes come first, then maze paths, then decay, then shear.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
public final class ShaftPainter {

    private static final BlockState AIR = Blocks.AIR.defaultBlockState();

    private final ShaftLayout layout;
    private final AeroliteSettings settings;
    private final ChunkAccess chunk;
    private final int minX;
    private final int minZ;
    private final BlockPos.MutableBlockPos cursor = new BlockPos.MutableBlockPos();
    /** The maze grid blocks of shafts and columns, outside the junction boxes. */
    private final LongSet shaftGrid = new LongOpenHashSet();
    /** The maze grid blocks of the patches, with the outward face direction. */
    private final Long2ObjectMap<Direction> patchGrid = new Long2ObjectOpenHashMap<>();
    private final Long2ObjectMap<MazeCarver.Carve> carves = new Long2ObjectOpenHashMap<>();

    private ShaftPainter(ShaftLayout layout, ChunkAccess chunk) {
        this.layout = layout;
        this.settings = layout.settings();
        this.chunk = chunk;
        ChunkPos pos = chunk.getPos();
        this.minX = pos.getMinBlockX();
        this.minZ = pos.getMinBlockZ();
    }

    public static void paint(ShaftLayout layout, ChunkAccess chunk) {
        new ShaftPainter(layout, chunk).paint();
    }

    private void paint() {
        int reach = Ring.BOX;
        List<ShaftLayout.Shaft> shafts = new ArrayList<>();
        shafts.addAll(this.layout.shafts(ShaftLayout.Axis.Z, this.minX - reach, this.minX + 15 + reach));
        shafts.addAll(this.layout.shafts(ShaftLayout.Axis.X, this.minZ - reach, this.minZ + 15 + reach));
        List<ShaftLayout.Column> columns = new ArrayList<>();
        for (ShaftLayout.Shaft zShaft : shafts) {
            if (zShaft.axis() != ShaftLayout.Axis.Z) {
                continue;
            }
            for (ShaftLayout.Shaft xShaft : shafts) {
                if (xShaft.axis() == ShaftLayout.Axis.X) {
                    this.layout.column(zShaft.cell(), xShaft.cell()).ifPresent(columns::add);
                }
            }
        }

        for (ShaftLayout.Shaft shaft : shafts) {
            this.fillShaft(shaft);
        }
        for (ShaftLayout.Column column : columns) {
            this.fillColumn(column);
        }
        for (ShaftLayout.Column column : columns) {
            this.fillBoxes(column);
        }

        for (ShaftLayout.Shaft shaft : shafts) {
            this.carveShaft(shaft);
        }
        for (ShaftLayout.Column column : columns) {
            MazeCarver.column(column, this.layout.columnMaze(column), this::carve);
        }

        this.decay();
        for (ShaftLayout.Column column : columns) {
            this.shear(column);
        }
    }

    private boolean inChunk(BlockPos pos) {
        return pos.getX() >= this.minX && pos.getX() < this.minX + 16
                && pos.getZ() >= this.minZ && pos.getZ() < this.minZ + 16
                && pos.getY() >= this.layout.minY() && pos.getY() < this.layout.maxY();
    }

    private void s
```

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.world;

import com.mojang.serialization.MapCodec;
import com.mojang.serialization.codecs.RecordCodecBuilder;
import net.minecraft.core.Holder;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.level.biome.BiomeSource;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.ChunkAccess;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.ChunkGenerator;
import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.NoiseBasedChunkGenerator;
import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.NoiseGeneratorSettings;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.ShaftLayout;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.ShaftPainter;

import javax.annotation.Nullable;

/**
 * The noise-based generator of the preset. It carries the settings and builds the shafts in their own chunk step.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
public class AeroliteChunkGenerator extends NoiseBasedChunkGenerator {

    public static final MapCodec<AeroliteChunkGenerator> CODEC = RecordCodecBuilder.mapCodec(instance -> instance.group(
            BiomeSource.CODEC.fieldOf("biome_source").forGetter(ChunkGenerator::getBiomeSource),
            NoiseGeneratorSettings.CODEC.fieldOf("settings").forGetter(NoiseBasedChunkGenerator::generatorSettings),
            AeroliteSettings.CODEC.optionalFieldOf("aerolite", AeroliteSettings.DEFAULT).forGetter(AeroliteChunkGenerator::aeroliteSettings)
    ).apply(instance, AeroliteChunkGenerator::new));

    private final AeroliteSettings aeroliteSettings;
    @Nullable
    private volatile CachedLayout cachedLayout;

    private record CachedLayout(long seed, String dimension, int minY, int maxY, ShaftLayout layout) {
    }

    public AeroliteChunkGenerator(BiomeSource biomeSource, Holder<NoiseGeneratorSettings> settings, AeroliteSettings aeroliteSettings) {
        super(biomeSource, settings);
        this.aeroliteSettings = aeroliteSettings;
    }

    @Override
    protected MapCodec<? extends ChunkGenerator> codec() {
        return CODEC;
    }

    public AeroliteSettings aeroliteSettings() {
        return this.aeroliteSettings;
    }

    public AeroliteChunkGenerator withAeroliteSettings(AeroliteSettings settings) {
        return new AeroliteChunkGenerator(this.getBiomeSource(), this.generatorSettings(), settings);
    }

    /** Writes the shafts, columns and junctions of one chunk. */
    public void buildShafts(ServerLevel level, ChunkAccess chunk) {
        ShaftPainter.paint(this.layout(level), chunk);
    }

    /** The shaft layout of the level. One generator serves one dimension, so the layout rarely changes. */
    public ShaftLayout layout(ServerLevel level) {
        long seed = level.getSeed();
        String dimension = level.dimension().location().toString();
        int minY = level.getMinBuildHeight();
        int maxY = level.getMaxBuildHeight();
        CachedLayout cached = this.cachedLayout;
        if (cached == null || cached.seed != seed || !cached.dimension.equals(dimension) || cached.minY != minY || cached.maxY != maxY) {
            cached = new CachedLayout(seed, dimension, minY, maxY, new ShaftLayout(seed, dimension, minY, maxY, this.aeroliteSettings));
            this.cachedLayout = cached;
        }
        return cached.layout;
    }

}
```

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.world.status;

import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.level.GenerationChunkHolder;
import net.minecraft.util.StaticCache2D;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.ChunkAccess;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.status.ChunkStatus;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.status.ChunkStep;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.status.WorldGenContext;
import org.jetbrains.annotations.ApiStatus;
import us.drullk.aerolite.Aerolite;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.AeroliteChunkGenerator;

import javax.annotation.Nullable;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;

/**
 * The shafts chunk status. It sits between the carvers and the features.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
public final class AeroliteChunkStatuses {

    public static final ResourceLocation SHAFTS_ID = ResourceLocation.fromNamespaceAndPath(Aerolite.MODID, "shafts");

    @Nullable
    private static ChunkStatus shafts;

    private AeroliteChunkStatuses() {
    }

    public static ChunkStatus shafts() {
        return Objects.requireNonNull(shafts, "The shafts chunk status is not registered");
    }

    /** Takes the status that the chunk status registration creates. */
    @ApiStatus.Internal
    public static void bind(ChunkStatus status) {
        shafts = status;
    }

    /** The generation task of the shafts step. */
    public static CompletableFuture<ChunkAccess> generateShafts(
            WorldGenContext context,
            ChunkStep step,
            StaticCache2D<GenerationChunkHolder> cache,
            ChunkAccess chunk
    ) {
        if (context.generator() instanceof AeroliteChunkGenerator generator) {
            generator.buildShafts(context.level(), chunk);
        }
        return CompletableFuture.completedFuture(chunk);
    }

}
```

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.mixin;

import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.status.ChunkStatus;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.status.ChunkType;
import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.Heightmap;
import org.objectweb.asm.Opcodes;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Final;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Shadow;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.ModifyArg;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Slice;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.status.AeroliteChunkStatuses;

import java.util.EnumSet;

/**
 * Registers the shafts chunk status as the parent of the features status.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
// No API adds a chunk status. Each status takes its index from its parent while the class initializes.
@Mixin(ChunkStatus.class)
abstract class ChunkStatusMixin {

    @Shadow
    @Final
    public static EnumSet<Heightmap.Types> FINAL_HEIGHTMAPS;

    @Shadow
    private static ChunkStatus register(String name, ChunkStatus parent, EnumSet<Heightmap.Types> heightmapsAfter, ChunkType chunkType) {
        throw new AssertionError();
    }

    @ModifyArg(
            method = "<clinit>",
            slice = @Slice(from = @At(value = "FIELD", target = "Lnet/minecraft/world/level/chunk/status/ChunkStatus;CARVERS:Lnet/minecraft/world/level/chunk/status/ChunkStatus;", opcode = Opcodes.PUTSTATIC)),
            at = @At(
                    value = "INVOKE",
                    target = "Lnet/minecraft/world/level/chunk/status/ChunkStatus;register(Ljava/lang/String;Lnet/minecraft/world/level/chunk/status/ChunkStatus;Ljava/util/EnumSet;Lnet/minecraft/world/level/chunk/status/ChunkType;)Lnet/minecraft/world/level/chunk/status/ChunkStatus;",
                    ordinal = 0
            ),
            index = 1
    )
    private static ChunkStatus aerolite$insertShafts(ChunkStatus carvers) {
        ChunkStatus shafts = register(AeroliteChunkStatuses.SHAFTS_ID.toString(), carvers, FINAL_HEIGHTMAPS, ChunkType.PROTOCHUNK);
        AeroliteChunkStatuses.bind(shafts);
        return shafts;
    }

}
```

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.mixin;

import com.llamalad7.mixinextras.injector.wrapoperation.Operation;
import com.llamalad7.mixinextras.injector.wrapoperation.WrapOperation;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.status.ChunkPyramid;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.status.ChunkStatus;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.status.ChunkStep;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Final;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Shadow;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.status.AeroliteChunkStatuses;

import java.util.function.UnaryOperator;

/**
 * Adds the shafts step before the features step of both chunk pyramids.
 * In the generation pyramid, the features step waits for the shafts of its neighbors.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
// No API adds a chunk step. The pyramids build their steps once while the class initializes.
@Mixin(ChunkPyramid.class)
abstract class ChunkPyramidMixin {

    @Shadow
    @Final
    public static ChunkPyramid GENERATION_PYRAMID;

    @WrapOperation(
            method = "<clinit>",
            at = @At(
                    value = "INVOKE",
                    target = "Lnet/minecraft/world/level/chunk/status/ChunkPyramid$Builder;step(Lnet/minecraft/world/level/chunk/status/ChunkStatus;Ljava/util/function/UnaryOperator;)Lnet/minecraft/world/level/chunk/status/ChunkPyramid$Builder;"
            )
    )
    private static ChunkPyramid.Builder aerolite$insertShaftsStep(
            ChunkPyramid.Builder builder,
            ChunkStatus status,
            UnaryOperator<ChunkStep.Builder> task,
            Operation<ChunkPyramid.Builder> original
    ) {
        if (status != ChunkStatus.FEATURES) {
            return original.call(builder, status, task);
        }
        ChunkStatus shafts = AeroliteChunkStatuses.shafts();
        // The generation pyramid builds first, so its field is still unset here.
        if (GENERATION_PYRAMID == null) {
            UnaryOperator<ChunkStep.Builder> shaftsTask = step -> step.blockStateWriteRadius(0).setTask(AeroliteChunkStatuses::generateShafts);
            UnaryOperator<ChunkStep.Builder> featuresTask = step -> task.apply(step).addRequirement(shafts, 1);
            return original.call(original.call(builder, shafts, shaftsTask), status, featuresTask);
        }
        return original.call(original.call(builder, shafts, UnaryOperator.<ChunkStep.Builder>identity()), status, task);
    }

}
```

```
public final class AeroliteWorldgen {
```

```
 */
@EventBusSubscriber(modid = Aerolite.MODID, value = Dist.CLIENT)
public final class AeroliteClient {

    private AeroliteClient() {
    }

    /** The customize button of the world creation menu opens the preset screen when the preset is active. */
    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void registerPresetEditors(RegisterPresetEditorsEvent event) {
        event.register(AeroliteWorldgen.PRESET, AerolitePresetScreen::new);
    }

}
```

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.datagen;

import net.minecraft.data.DataGenerator;
import net.minecraft.data.PackOutput;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.EventBusSubscriber;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.data.event.GatherDataEvent;
import us.drullk.aerolite.Aerolite;

/**
 * Adds the data providers of Aerolite to the data run.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_06-17_8d424d05-8940-4aa5-872e-7b2bd927cb73.md}.
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
@EventBusSubscriber(modid = Aerolite.MODID)
public final class AeroliteDataGenerators {

    private AeroliteDataGenerators() {
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void gatherData(GatherDataEvent event) {
        DataGenerator generator = event.getGenerator();
        PackOutput output = generator.getPackOutput();

        generator.addProvider(event.includeClient(), new AeroliteLanguageProvider(output));

        AeroliteWorldgenProvider worldgen = generator.addProvider(event.includeServer(), new AeroliteWorldgenProvider(output, event.getLookupProvider()));
        generator.addProvider(event.includeServer(), new AeroliteWorldPresetTagsProvider(output, worldgen.getRegistryProvider(), event.getExistingFileHelper()));
    }

}
```

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze;

import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotEquals;

/**
 * Confirms that a set of maze edges forms one tree.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
final class TreeCheck {

    private final Map<MazeNode, MazeNode> parent = new HashMap<>();

    private TreeCheck() {
    }

    private MazeNode find(MazeNode node) {
        MazeNode root = node;
        while (true) {
            MazeNode next = this.parent.getOrDefault(root, root);
            if (next.equals(root)) {
                break;
            }
            root = next;
        }
        this.parent.put(node, root);
        return root;
    }

    /** Asserts no loop and one component. Returns the node count. */
    static int assertOneTree(Collection<MazeEdge> edges) {
        TreeCheck check = new TreeCheck();
        Set<MazeNode> nodes = new HashSet<>();
        for (MazeEdge edge : edges) {
            nodes.add(edge.a());
            nodes.add(edge.b());
            MazeNode a = check.find(edge.a());
            MazeNode b = check.find(edge.b());
            assertNotEquals(a, b, () -> "The edge " + edge + " closes a loop");
            check.parent.put(a, b);
        }
        Set<MazeNode> roots = new HashSet<>();
        for (MazeNode node : nodes) {
            roots.add(check.find(node));
        }
        assertEquals(1, roots.size(), "The edges form more than one component");
        assertEquals(nodes.size() - 1, edges.size());
        return nodes.size();
    }

}
```

</details>

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze;

import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.XoroshiroRandomSource;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/**
 * Checks the segments of a horizontal shaft maze.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
class ShaftMazeTest {

    private static ShaftMaze maze(long seed, List<ShaftMaze.Box> boxes) {
        ShaftMaze.BoxLookup lookup = (min, max) -> boxes.stream().filter(box -> box.center() >= min && box.center() <= max).toList();
        return new ShaftMaze(lookup, new XoroshiroRandomSource(seed).forkPositional());
    }

    /** The distinct segments from the first chunk to the last chunk, by first chunk. */
    private static Map<Integer, ShaftMaze.Segment> segments(ShaftMaze maze, int firstChunk, int lastChunk) {
        Map<Integer, ShaftMaze.Segment> segments = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        for (int chunk = firstChunk; chunk <= lastChunk; chunk++) {
            ShaftMaze.Segment segment = maze.segmentAt(chunk);
            segments.putIfAbsent(segment.firstChunk(), segment);
        }
        return segments;
    }

    private static List<MazeEdge> edges(Map<Integer, ShaftMaze.Segment> segments) {
        List<MazeEdge> edges = new ArrayList<>();
        segments.values().forEach(segment -> edges.addAll(segment.edges()));
        return edges;
    }

    @Test
    void ringBlocksRoundTrip() {
        for (int block = 0; block < Ring.BLOCKS; block++) {
            assertEquals(block, Ring.block(Ring.first(block), Ring.second(block)));
        }
    }

    @Test
    void plainShaftIsOneTree() {
        for (long seed = 0; seed < 4; seed++) {
            TreeCheck.assertOneTree(edges(segments(maze(seed, List.of()), 0, 10)));
        }
    }

    @Test
    void plainShaftIsOneTreeAtNegativeChunks() {
        TreeCheck.assertOneTree(edges(segments(maze(7, List.of()), -12, -2)));
    }

    @Test
    void connectorHasCrossingsOnBothSides() {
        ShaftMaze.Segment connector = maze(3, List.of()).segmentAt(5);
        assertTrue(connector.edges().stream().anyMatch(edge -> edge.a() instanceof MazeNode.Ruling a && a.along() == 16 * 5 - 1
                || edge.b() instanceof MazeNode.Ruling b && b.along() == 16 * 5 - 1));
        assertTrue(connector.edges().stream().anyMatch(edge -> edge.a() instanceof MazeNode.Ruling a && a.along() == 16 * 6 + 1
                || edge.b() instanceof MazeNode.Ruling b && b.along() == 16 * 6 + 1));
    }

    @Test
    void junctionShaftIsOneTree() {
        for (int center = 160; center < 192; center++) {
            for (int sign : new int[]{-1, 1}) {
                ShaftMaze maze = maze(center, List.of(new ShaftMaze.Box(center, sign)));
                TreeCheck.assertOneTree(edges(segments(maze, 4, 18)));
            }
        }
    }

    @Test
    void closeBoxesShareOneJunctionSegment() {
        List<ShaftMaze.Box> boxes = List.of(new ShaftMaze.Box(184, 1), new ShaftMaze.Box(215, -1), new ShaftMaze.Box(246, 1));
        ShaftMaze maze = maze(11, boxes);
        ShaftMaze.Segment segment = maze.segmentAt(12);
        assertEquals(3, segment.boxes().size());
        assertEquals(segment.edges(), maze.segmentAt(segment.lastChunk()).edges());
        TreeCheck.assertOneTree(edges(segments(maze, 6, 20)));
    }

    @Test
    void segmentsArePure() {
        ShaftMaze first = maze(5, List.of(new ShaftMaze.Box(300, 1)));
        ShaftMaze second = maze(5, List.of(new ShaftMaze.Box(300, 1)));
        for (int chunk = 14; chunk <= 22; chunk++) {
            assertEquals(first.segmentAt(chunk), second.segmentAt(chunk));
        }
    }

    void junctionDropsTheBoxRulings() {
        ShaftMaze.Box box = new
```

```
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/**
 * Checks the pieces and column patches of a column maze.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
class ColumnMazeTest {

    private static List<MazeEdge> allEdges(ColumnMaze maze) {
        List<MazeEdge> edges = new ArrayList<>();
        maze.pieces().forEach(piece -> edges.addAll(piece.edges()));
        maze.patches().forEach(patch -> edges.addAll(patch.edges()));
        return edges;
    }

    @Test
    void columnIsOneTree() {
        for (long seed = 0; seed < 4; seed++) {
            List<ColumnMaze.Junction> junctions = List.of(new ColumnMaze.Junction(7, 1, true), new ColumnMaze.Junction(120, -1, false));
            ColumnMaze maze = ColumnMaze.build(-64, 320, junctions, new XoroshiroRandomSource(seed).forkPositional());
            assertEquals(3, maze.pieces().size());
            TreeCheck.assertOneTree(allEdges(maze));
        }
    }

    @Test
    void junctionAtTheBottomLimitLeavesAnEmptyPiece() {
        List<ColumnMaze.Junction> junctions = List.of(new ColumnMaze.Junction(7, 1, true), new ColumnMaze.Junction(60, 1, false));
        ColumnMaze maze = ColumnMaze.build(0, 256, junctions, new XoroshiroRandomSource(2).forkPositional());
        assertTrue(maze.pieces().getFirst().isEmpty());
        TreeCheck.assertOneTree(allEdges(maze));
    }

    @Test
    void oneRulingFitsBetweenJunctions() {
        assertTrue(ColumnMaze.fits(List.of(new ColumnMaze.Junction(23, 1, true), new ColumnMaze.Junction(40, 1, false))));
        assertFalse(ColumnMaze.fits(List.of(new ColumnMaze.Junction(23, 1, true), new ColumnMaze.Junction(38, 1, false))));
    }

    @Test
    void columnNodesFaceThePatch() {
        ColumnMaze.Junction alongX = new ColumnMaze.Junction(0, 1, true);
        ColumnMaze.Junction alongZ = new ColumnMaze.Junction(0, -1, false);
        for (int i = -2; i <= 2; i++) {
            int x = alongX.columnNode(i) * 2;
            assertEquals(2 * i, Ring.first(x));
            assertEquals(Ring.OUTER, Ring.second(x));
            int z = alongZ.columnNode(i) * 2;
            assertEquals(-Ring.OUTER, Ring.first(z));
            assertEquals(2 * i, Ring.second(z));
        }
    }

}
```

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.AeroliteSettings;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze.ColumnMaze;

import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.stream.IntStream;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/**
 * Checks the placement of shafts and columns.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
class ShaftLayoutTest {

    private static final int MIN_Y = -64;
    private static final int MAX_Y = 320;

    private static ShaftLayout layout(long seed, int spacing) {
        AeroliteSettings settings = new AeroliteSettings(spacing, 0.25F, 256, 0.5F,
                AeroliteSettings.DEFAULT.interiorBlock(), AeroliteSettings.DEFAULT.wallBlock(), AeroliteSettings.DEFAULT.pathBlock());
        return new ShaftLayout(seed, "minecraft:overworld", MIN_Y, MAX_Y, settings);
    }

    @Test
    void slotsSplitBetweenTheAxes() {
        ShaftLayout layout = layout(1, 256);
        int[] x = layout.slots(ShaftLayout.Axis.X);
        int[] z = layout.slots(ShaftLayout.Axis.Z);
        assertEquals(12, x.length);
        assertEquals(12, z.length);
        int[] all = IntStream.concat(Arrays.stream(x), Arrays.stream(z)).sorted().toArray();
        assertTrue(Arrays.equals(IntStream.range(0, 24).toArray(), all));
    }

    @Test
    void neighborShaftsKeepSixteenBlocksApart() {
        for (int spacing : new int[]{32, 64, 256}) {
            ShaftLayout layout = layout(spacing, spacing);
            for (ShaftLayout.Axis axis : ShaftLayout.Axis.values()) {
                List<ShaftLayout.Shaft> shafts = layout.shafts(axis, -20 * spacing, 20 * spacing);
                for (int k = 1; k < shafts.size(); k++) {
                    int gap = shafts.get(k).position() - shafts.get(k - 1).position() - (2 * ShaftLayout.RADIUS + 1);
                    assertTrue(gap >= 16, "gap " + gap);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    @Test
    void shaftsStayInTheirSlot() {
        ShaftLayout layout = layout(3, 256);
        for (ShaftLayout.Axis axis : ShaftLayout.Axis.values()) {
            int[] slots = layout.slots(axis);
            for (ShaftLayout.Shaft shaft : layout.shafts(axis, -10000, 10000)) {
                int bottom = shaft.centerY() - ShaftLayout.RADIUS - MIN_Y;
                int slot = bottom / ShaftLayout.SLOT;
                int offset = bottom % ShaftLayout.SLOT;
                assertTrue(Arrays.stream(slots).anyMatch(s -> s == slot));
                assertTrue(offset == 1 || offset == 2);
            }
        }
    }

    @Test
    void columnsHaveRoomBetweenTheirJunctions() {
        ShaftLayout layout = layout(4, 64);
        int found = 0;
        for (int xCell = -20; xCell < 20; xCell++) {
            for (int zCell = -20; zCell < 20; zCell++) {
                Optional<ShaftLayout.Column> column = layout.column(xCell, zCell);
                if (column.isPresent()) {
                    found++;
                    assertTrue(ColumnMaze.fits(column.get().junctions()));
                    assertTrue(column.get().bottomShear() >= ShaftLayout.MIN_SHEAR && column.get().bottomShear() <= ShaftLayout.MAX_SHEAR);
                }
            }
        }
        assertTrue(found > 0);
    }

    @Test
    void layoutIsPure() {
        assertEquals(layout(9, 256).shafts(ShaftLayout.Axis.X, -4000, 4000), layout(9, 256).shafts(ShaftLayout.Axis.X, -4000, 4000));
    }

}
```

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.world;

import com.google.gson.JsonElement;
import com.google.gson.JsonParser;
import com.mojang.serialization.JsonOps;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/**
 * Checks the codec of the preset settings.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
class AeroliteSettingsTest {

    @Test
    void settingsRoundTrip() {
        AeroliteSettings settings = AeroliteSettings.DEFAULT.withDecayScale(64).withDecayThreshold(0.35F);
        JsonElement json = AeroliteSettings.CODEC.encodeStart(JsonOps.INSTANCE, settings).getOrThrow();
        assertEquals(settings, AeroliteSettings.CODEC.parse(JsonOps.INSTANCE, json).getOrThrow());
    }

    @Test
    void missingFieldsTakeTheDefaults() {
        assertEquals(AeroliteSettings.DEFAULT, AeroliteSettings.CODEC.parse(JsonOps.INSTANCE, JsonParser.parseString("{}")).getOrThrow());
    }

    @Test
    void decayScaleIsAPowerOfTwo() {
        assertTrue(AeroliteSettings.CODEC.parse(JsonOps.INSTANCE, JsonParser.parseString("{\"decay_scale\": 48}")).isError());
        assertTrue(AeroliteSettings.CODEC.parse(JsonOps.INSTANCE, JsonParser.parseString("{\"decay_scale\": 2048}")).isError());
    }

}
```

```bash
package us.drullk.aerolite.gametest;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries;
import net.minecraft.gametest.framework.GameTest;
import net.minecraft.gametest.framework.GameTestHelper;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.level.ChunkPos;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.ProtoChunk;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.UpgradeData;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.gametest.GameTestHolder;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.gametest.PrefixGameTestTemplate;
import us.drullk.aerolite.Aerolite;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.AeroliteSettings;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.ShaftLayout;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.ShaftPainter;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze.ColumnMaze;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

@GameTestHolder(Aerolite.MODID)
@PrefixGameTestTemplate(false)
public final class JunctionDumpGameTests {
    @GameTest(template = "empty")
    public static void dumpJunction(GameTestHelper helper) throws IOException {
        ServerLevel level = helper.getLevel();
        float threshold = Float.parseFloat(System.getProperty("aerolite.dumpThreshold", "1"));
        AeroliteSettings settings = AeroliteSettings.DEFAULT.withDecayThreshold(threshold).withDecayScale(32);
        ShaftLayout layout = new ShaftLayout(42, "test", level.getMinBuildHeight(), level.getMaxBuildHeight(), settings);
        ShaftLayout.Column column = null;
        for (int i = 0; i < 40 && column == null; i++) for (int j = 0; j < 40 && column == null; j++) {
            Optional<ShaftLayout.Column> c = layout.column(i, j);
            if (c.isPresent()) column = c.get();
        }
        int reach = 24;
        List<String> lines = new ArrayList<>();
        lines.add("column " + column.x() + " " + column.z() + " " + column.junctions());
        for (int cx = (column.x() - reach) >> 4; cx <= (column.x() + reach) >> 4; cx++) {
            for (int cz = (column.z() - reach) >> 4; cz <= (column.z() + reach) >> 4; cz++) {
                ChunkPos pos = new ChunkPos(cx, cz);
                ProtoChunk chunk = new ProtoChunk(pos, UpgradeData.EMPTY, level, level.registryAccess().registryOrThrow(Registries.BIOME), null);
                ShaftPainter.paint(layout, chunk);
                for (ColumnMaze.Junction junction : column.junctions()) {
                    for (int x = pos.getMinBlockX(); x <= pos.getMaxBlockX(); x++) for (int z = pos.getMinBlockZ(); z <= pos.getMaxBlockZ(); z++) {
                        if (Math.abs(x - column.x()) > reach || Math.abs(z - column.z()) > reach) continue;
                        for (int y = junction.center() - reach; y <= junction.center() + reach; y++) {
                            BlockState s = chunk.getBlockState(new BlockPos(x, y, z));
                            int code = s.isAir() ? 0 : s == settings.interiorBlock() ? 1 : s == settings.wallBlock() ? 2 : s == settings.pathBlock() ? 3 : 9;
                            if (code != 0) lines.add(junction.center() + " " + x + " " + y + " " + z + " " + code);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
```

<details>
<summary>**Tool call: `Bash`** — Scan saved chunks for statuses and blocks</summary>

```bash
D=/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-drullkus-IdeaProjects-aerolite/5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6/scratchpad; cat > $D/region_scan.py <<'EOF'
import sys, struct, zlib, glob, collections, os
def nbt(b, i, t):
    if t==1: return b[i], i+1
    if t==2: return struct.unpack('>h',b[i:i+2])[0], i+2
    if t==3: return struct.unpack('>i',b[i:i+4])[0], i+4
    if t==4: return struct.unpack('>q',b[i:i+8])[0], i+8
    if t==5: return None, i+4
    if t==6: return None, i+8
    if t==7: n=struct.unpack('>i',b[i:i+4])[0]; return None, i+4+n
    if t==8: n=struct.unpack('>H',b[i:i+2])[0]; return b[i+2:i+2+n].decode('utf8','replace'), i+2+n
    if t==9:
        et=b[i]; n=struct.unpack('>i',b[i+1:i+5])[0]; i+=5; out=[]
        for _ in range(n): v,i=nbt(b,i,et); out.append(v)
        return out, i
    if t==10:
        d={}
        while True:
            tt=b[i]; i+=1
            if tt==0: return d, i
            n=struct.unpack('>H',b[i:i+2])[0]; name=b[i+2:i+2+n].decode(); i+=2+n
            d[name], i = nbt(b,i,tt)
    if t==11: n=struct.unpack('>i',b[i:i+4])[0]; return None, i+4+4*n
    if t==12: n=struct.unpack('>i',b[i:i+4])[0]; return None, i+4+8*n
statuses=collections.Counter(); blocks=collections.Counter(); chunks_with_shaft=0
for f in glob.glob(sys.argv[1]+'/*.mca'):
    data=open(f,'rb').read()
    for k in range(1024):
        off=int.from_bytes(data[4*k:4*k+3],'big')
        if not off: continue
        p=off*4096; ln=struct.unpack('>i',data[p:p+4])[0]
        root,_=nbt(zlib.decompress(data[p+5:p+4+ln]),3,10)
        statuses[root.get('Status')]+=1
        names=set()
        for s in root.get('sections',[]):
            for e in s.get('block_states',{}).get('palette',[]): names.add(e['Name'])
        for n in names: blocks[n]+=1
        if 'minecraft:stone_bricks' in names: chunks_with_shaft+=1
print('statuses', dict(statuses)); print('chunks per block', dict(blocks)); print('chunks with maze walls', chunks_with_shaft)
EOF
for d in region DIM-1/region DIM1/region; do echo "== $d"; python3 -I $D/region_scan.py /Users/drullkus/IdeaProjects/aerolite/.claude/worktrees/world-preset/run/aerolite_check/$d; done
```

</details>

```bash
by=collections.defaultdict(collections.Counter); ys=collections.Counter(); pos=[]
for f in glob.glob(sys.argv[1]+'/*.mca'):
    data=open(f,'rb').read()
    for k in range(1024):
        off=int.from_bytes(data[4*k:4*k+3],'big')
        if not off: continue
        p=off*4096; ln=struct.unpack('>i',data[p:p+4])[0]
        root,_=nbt(zlib.decompress(data[p+5:p+4+ln]),3,10)
        for s in root.get('sections',[]):
            pal=[e['Name'] for e in s.get('block_states',{}).get('palette',[])]
            for n in pal:

        if root.get('Status')=='minecraft:full': pos.append((root['xPos'],root['zPos']))
print(dict(by)); print(sorted(ys.items())[:12])
print('full chunk x range', min(p[0] for p in pos), max(p[0] for p in pos), 'z range', min(p[1] for p in pos), max(p[1] for p in pos))
EOF
```

<details>
<summary>**Tool call: `Bash`** — Record the session decisions in CLAUDE.md</summary>

```bash
python3 - CLAUDE.md <<'EOF'
import sys
p=sys.argv[1]; s=open(p).read()
def rep(a,b):
    global s
    assert a in s, a
    s=s.replace(a,b,1)
rep("""- Each dimension has the same structure: shafts, islands, bridges and center structures.
""","""- Each dimension has the same structure: shafts, islands, bridges and center structures.
- Each dimension uses the Aerolite generator, a subclass of the vanilla noise-based chunk generator. It carries the settings.
- The noise settings of each dimension make only air. The islands replace the void later.
- The void Overworld has a sea level of -64. A higher sea level makes the noise step fill the bottom with lava.
- The shafts are the chunk status `aerolite:shafts`, between the carvers and the features. Two mixins add the status and its step, because no API adds a chunk status.
- The features step waits for the shafts of its neighbor chunks. Features decorate the shafts.
""")
rep("""| Maze decay scale | 256 blocks |
""","""| Maze decay scale | 256 blocks |
| Maze decay threshold | 0.5 |
""")
rep("""- The maze decay scale is the size of one decay region along a shaft.
""","""- The maze decay scale is the size of one decay region along a shaft.
- The customize button opens the Aerolite screen when the preset is active. The screen edits every Aerolite dimension.
- The screen has two sliders: the maze decay scale and the maze decay threshold.
- The decay scale slider steps through the powers of two from 32 to 1024.
- The decay threshold slider runs from 0 to 1 in steps of 0.05.
- The other settings have no slider yet. They live in the generator settings with their defaults.
""")
rep("""- An X shaft and a Z shaft never intersect.
""","""- An X shaft and a Z shaft never intersect.
- Each axis is a jittered grid. Each cell of the shaft spacing holds one shaft at a seeded place.
- A margin in each cell keeps at least 16 blocks between two shafts on the same axis.
""")
rep("""- Columns span the full build height.
""","""- A crossing gets no column when no ruling fits between its two junction boxes.
- Columns span the full build height.
""")
rep("""- Near a build limit, a noise threshold removes blocks from the column. The threshold rises toward the limit, so fewer blocks remain the closer they are to it.
""","""- Near a build limit, a noise threshold removes blocks from the column. The threshold rises toward the limit, so fewer blocks remain the closer they are to it.
- Each column end picks its shear band height at random, from 8 to 64 blocks.
- Shear does not touch a junction box.
""")
rep("""- The junction segment keeps the chunk alternation. An end chunk with an odd index gives it a virtual node on that side.
""","""- The junction segment keeps the chunk alternation. An end chunk with an odd index gives it a virtual node on that side.
- When the junction segments of two boxes share a chunk, one junction segment spans both boxes. It holds one shaft patch per box.
""")
rep("""- A maze wall or maze path block that decay removes becomes air. A removed path exposes the interior block.
""","""- The decay threshold t sets the regimes. Noise below -t keeps only maze walls. Noise above t keeps only maze paths.
- A maze wall or maze path block that decay removes becomes air. A removed path exposes the interior block.
- Decay acts on the 13x13 inside of each patch. On a patch, the box block that decay exposes becomes the maze path block.
- The rim of a patch face stays wall. Decay does not touch it.
""")
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
```

```bash
The preset gives the Overworld, the Nether and the End a noise-based
Aerolite generator with void noise settings. The generator carries the
settings. A new chunk status, aerolite:shafts, sits between the carvers
and the features and writes the shafts, columns, junctions and their
maze, with decay and shear. The customize screen of the world creation
menu has sliders for the maze decay scale and the maze decay threshold.
```

### Claude:

I added the Aerolite world preset and the shafts chunk step, and committed them on `claude/world-preset`. The customize screen with the two decay sliders compiles and the client reaches the main menu with it. I have not clicked through that screen, because I can't drive the client GUI from here.

**What it does**
- **Preset:** `aerolite:aerolite` is in `#minecraft:normal`. The Overworld, the Nether and the End each get `AeroliteChunkGenerator`, a subclass of the noise-based chunk generator. The generator carries the settings.
- **Terrain:** the noise settings make only air, as you chose.
- **Chunk step:** two mixins put `aerolite:shafts` between carvers and features in both chunk pyramids. Features wait for the shafts of their neighbor chunks, so ores and other features land inside the shafts. No API adds a chunk status, so mixins are the only way.
- **Shafts:**
  - Placement uses a jittered grid, with at least 16 blocks between two shafts on the same axis.
  - A crossing gets no column when no ruling fits between its two junction boxes.
  - Each column end gets a random shear band of 8 to 64 blocks.
  - The full maze is in: segments, junction segments, column pieces and patches.
- **Decay:** the threshold t is a setting, default 0.5. On a patch, the box block that decay exposes becomes cobblestone.
- **Sliders:** the customize screen has a decay scale slider (powers of two from 32 to 1024) and a threshold slider (0 to 1 in steps of 0.05).

**Choices you did not make**
- **Patch decay reading:** I took "place cobblestone instead of junction block" to mean the exposed box block becomes the path block. I also kept the one-block rim of each patch face as wall, so decay does not touch it.
- **Merged junction segments:** with only a 16-block gap, the junction segments of two columns on one shaft can share a chunk. Then one junction segment spans both boxes. I added this case to the prototype first (`junction.py --cluster-stats`). All 600 random scenes stayed one tree.
- **Overworld sea level:** the first test world had a lava floor at the bottom. Vanilla fills air below Y -54 with lava when the sea level is higher than that. I set the void Overworld's sea level to -64, the same value vanilla's floating islands preset uses.
- **Small constants:** decay is 4 octaves of simplex noise. Shear noise is 3 octaves at a 16-block scale.

These decisions are now in CLAUDE.md.

**Test results**
- **Build:** compile and the full build pass, and data generation writes the expected files. No test or data generation classes are in the production jar.
- **JUnit:** 20 tests pass. They cover one tree per shaft, column and merged cluster, the 16-block gap, the slots and the settings codec.
- **GameTests:** 3 pass. They check the step order, the blocks the painter writes, and that the preset loads from the data pack and builds shafts.
- **Real world:** a dev-server world with the preset generated its spawn area in about 2 seconds. 58 saved chunks hold shafts at the `aerolite:shafts` status. That run left a `server.properties` set to the preset in the worktree's `run/` folder, which is gitignored.
- **Renders:** I rendered junctions from the real painter. They match the prototype images, and decay shows on the shafts and the patches.

**Not done yet**
- The player spawns in the void and falls.
- The Overworld biome is the same everywhere, because the void gives the climate noise nothing to vary.
- Vanilla structures still place wherever their rules allow, until the center structure work.

Apply the branch to `main` without a commit:

```bash
git cherry-pick -n main..claude/world-preset
```
