```bash
package us.drullk.aerolite.gametest;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries;
import net.minecraft.gametest.framework.GameTest;
import net.minecraft.gametest.framework.GameTestHelper;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.level.ChunkPos;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.ProtoChunk;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.UpgradeData;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.gametest.GameTestHolder;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.gametest.PrefixGameTestTemplate;
import us.drullk.aerolite.Aerolite;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.AeroliteSettings;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.ShaftLayout;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.ShaftPainter;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

@GameTestHolder(Aerolite.MODID)
@PrefixGameTestTemplate(false)
public final class ColumnDumpGameTests {
    @GameTest(template = "empty")
    public static void dumpColumns(GameTestHelper helper) throws IOException {
        ServerLevel level = helper.getLevel();
        long seed = -5285247543767198111L;
        ShaftLayout layout = new ShaftLayout(seed, "minecraft:overworld", -64, 320, AeroliteSettings.DEFAULT);
        List<String> lines = new ArrayList<>();
        int[][] spots = {{-79, -1617}, {1702, 1110}};
        for (int[] spot : spots) {
            ShaftLayout.Column best = null;
            double bestDistance = Double.MAX_VALUE;
            int s = 256;
            for (int i = Math.floorDiv(spot[0], s) - 2; i <= Math.floorDiv(spot[0], s) + 2; i++) for (int j = Math.floorDiv(spot[1], s) - 2; j <= Math.floorDiv(spot[1], s) + 2; j++) {
                Optional<ShaftLayout.Column> c = layout.column(i, j);
                if (c.isPresent()) {
                    double d = Math.hypot(c.get().x() - spot[0], c.get().z() - spot[1]);
                    if (d < bestDistance) { bestDistance = d; best = c.get(); }
                }
            }
            lines.add("column " + spot[0] + " " + spot[1] + " -> " + best);
            for (int cx = (best.x() - 8) >> 4; cx <= (best.x() + 8) >> 4; cx++) for (int cz = (best.z() - 8) >> 4; cz <= (best.z() + 8) >> 4; cz++) {
                ChunkPos pos = new ChunkPos(cx, cz);
                ProtoChunk chunk = new ProtoChunk(pos, UpgradeData.EMPTY, level, level.registryAccess().registryOrThrow(Registries.BIOME), null);
                ShaftPainter.paint(layout, chunk);
                for (int x = pos.getMinBlockX(); x <= pos.getMaxBlockX(); x++) for (int z = pos.getMinBlockZ(); z <= pos.getMaxBlockZ(); z++) {
                    if (Math.abs(x - best.x()) > 8 || Math.abs(z - best.z()) > 8) continue;
                    for (int y = -64; y < 320; y++) {
                        BlockState b = chunk.getBlockState(new BlockPos(x, y, z));
                        int code = b.isAir() ? 0 : b == AeroliteSettings.DEFAULT.interiorBlock() ? 1 : b == AeroliteSettings.DEFAULT.wallBlock() ? 2 : b == AeroliteSettings.DEFAULT.pathBlock() ? 3 : 9;
                        if (code != 0) lines.add(spot[0] + " " + (x - best.x()) + " " + y + " " + (z - best.z()) + " " + code);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        Files.write(Path.of("column_dump.txt"), lines);
        helper.succeed();
    }
}
EOF
```

```bash
        ShaftLayout.Column column = layout.column(-1, -7).orElseThrow();
        ColumnMaze maze = layout.columnMaze(column);
        for (ColumnMaze.Piece piece : maze.pieces()) {
            long along = piece.edges().stream().filter(e -> e.a() instanceof MazeNode.Ruling a && e.b() instanceof MazeNode.Ruling b && a.along() != b.along()).count();
            System.out.println("PROBE piece " + piece.first() + ".." + piece.last() + " edges " + piece.edges().size() + " along " + along);

        for (int rulings : new int[]{8, 48, 128, 190}) {
            int[] r = new int[rulings];
            for (int u = 0; u < rulings; u++) r[u] = 2 * u + 1;
            for (long seed = 0; seed < 3; seed++) {
                RulingGraph g = new RulingGraph(r);
                int[] parent = g.graph.sampleTree(new XoroshiroRandomSource(seed).forkPositional().at(0, 2, 0));
                int alongEdges = 0;
                for (int n = 0; n < parent.length; n++) if (parent[n] >= 0 && Math.abs(n - parent[n]) == Ring.NODES) alongEdges++;
                System.out.println("PROBE rulings " + rulings + " seed " + seed + " along " + alongEdges + " of " + (parent.length - 1));
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```bash
    """One full-height column cut into 16-block sections, like a horizontal shaft.

    Tree sections and connector sections alternate. A virtual node only faces a

    patch, with crossings from the patch border onto the nearest rulings.

    def __init__(self, seed, centers, signs, y_min, y_max, sampler, steps_per_node):
        self.centers, self.signs = centers, signs
        self.y_min, self.y_max = y_min, y_max
        self.s_lo, self.s_hi = y_min // CHUNK, (y_max - 1) // CHUNK
        self.sampler, self.spn = sampler, steps_per_node
        ranges = sorted(junction_chunks(c) for c in centers)
        clusters = []
        for lo, hi in ranges:
            if clusters and lo <= clusters[-1][1]:
                clusters[-1][1] = max(clusters[-1][1], hi)
            else:
                clusters.append([lo, hi])
        self.edges = []
        self.rulings = []
        covered = set()
        for lo, hi in clusters:
            lo, hi = max(lo, self.s_lo), min(hi, self.s_hi)
            covered.update(range(lo, hi + 1))
            boxes = [(c, g) for c, g in zip(centers, signs) if lo <= junction_chunks(c)[0] <= hi or lo <= junction_chunks(c)[1] <= hi]
            self._segment(lo, hi, boxes, seg_rng(seed, "column-junction", lo))
        for c in range(self.s_lo, self.s_hi + 1):
            if c not in covered:
                self._segment(c, c, [], seg_rng(seed, "column", c))

        g = Graph()
        names = {}
        index = {}
        rulings = [y for y in range(CHUNK * lo + 1, CHUNK * (hi + 1), 2) if all(abs(y - c) > HALF for c, _ in boxes)]
        self.rulings += rulings
        for y in rulings:
            for v in range(RING):
                n = g.add_node()
                index[(y, v)] = n
                names[n] = (y, v)
```

```bash
                case COLUMN_ALONG_X -> Ring.block(2 * k, Ring.OUTER * this.sign) / 2;

            };

        int patchNode(int firstPatchNode, int along, int across) {

                    : RulingGraph.patchNode(firstPatchNode, across, along);
        }
```

### Claude:

Now `ColumnMaze` gives way to a small `ColumnJunction` record. The column maze itself becomes a bounded `ShaftMaze`.

<details>
<summary>**Tool call: `Bash`** — Add ColumnJunction and list call sites to update</summary>

```bash
git rm -q src/main/java/us/drullk/aerolite/world/shaft/maze/ColumnMaze.java && cat > src/main/java/us/drullk/aerolite/world/shaft/maze/ColumnJunction.java <<'EOF'
package us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * A junction on a column.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 *
 * @param center the Y of the junction center
 * @param sign   the sign of the column patch face, along the second offset of the horizontal shaft
 * @param alongX true when the horizontal shaft runs along X
 */
public record ColumnJunction(int center, int sign, boolean alongX) {

    /** The box of this junction in the column maze. */
    public ShaftMaze.Box box() {
        return new ShaftMaze.Box(this.center, this.sign, this.alongX ? ShaftMaze.Patching.COLUMN_ALONG_X : ShaftMaze.Patching.COLUMN_ALONG_Z);
    }

    /**
     * True when at least one ruling fits between each two neighbor junctions.
     *
     * @param junctions the junctions in ascending sequence of Y
     */
    public static boolean fits(List<ColumnJunction> junctions) {
        for (int k = 1; k < junctions.size(); k++) {
            if (junctions.get(k - 1).box().firstRulingAfter() > junctions.get(k).box().lastRulingBefore()) {
                return false;
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

}
EOF
```

### Claude:

Now the carver. One segment routine serves shafts and columns, with a face for each box.

<details>
<summary>**Tool call: `Bash`** — Generalize segment carving to columns</summary>

```bash
python3 - src/main/java/us/drullk/aerolite/world/shaft/MazeCarver.java <<'EOF'
import sys
p=sys.argv[1]; s=open(p).read()
def rep(a,b):
    global s
    assert a in s, a
    s=s.replace(a,b,1)
rep("import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze.ColumnMaze;\n","")
start=s.index("    /** Carves the edges and patch nodes of one horizontal shaft segment. */")
end=s.index("    private static void patchNodes(")
s=s[:start]+'''    /** Carves the edges and patch nodes of one horizontal shaft segment. */
    static void shaftSegment(ShaftFrame frame, ShaftMaze.Segment segment, Consumer<Carve> out) {
        segment(frame, segment, box -> new PatchFace(frame, box.sign(), box.center()), out);
    }

    /** Carves the edges and column patch nodes of one column segment. */
    static void columnSegment(ShaftLayout.Column column, ShaftMaze.Segment segment, Consumer<Carve> out) {
        segment(ShaftFrame.of(column), segment, box -> columnPatchFace(column, box), out);
    }

    private static void segment(ShaftFrame frame, ShaftMaze.Segment segment, Function<ShaftMaze.Box, PatchFace> faceOf, Consumer<Carve> out) {
        Map<Integer, PatchFace> faces = segment.boxes().stream().collect(Collectors.toMap(ShaftMaze.Box::center, faceOf));
        for (PatchFace face : faces.values()) {
            patchNodes(face, out);
        }
        rulings(frame, segment.rulings(), segment.edges(), out);
        edges(frame, segment.edges(), faces::get, out);
    }

    /** The column patch face of a junction box, in the frame of its horizontal shaft. */
    static PatchFace columnPatchFace(ShaftLayout.Column column, ShaftMaze.Box box) {
        return box.patching() == ShaftMaze.Patching.COLUMN_ALONG_X
                ? new PatchFace(new ShaftFrame(ShaftFrame.Kind.ALONG_X, box.center(), column.z()), box.sign(), column.x())
                : new PatchFace(new ShaftFrame(ShaftFrame.Kind.ALONG_Z, box.center(), column.x()), box.sign(), column.z());
    }

'''+s[end:]
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
```

<details>
<summary>**Tool call: `Bash`** — Update the tests and run JUnit</summary>

```bash
cat > src/test/java/us/drullk/aerolite/world/shaft/maze/ColumnMazeTest.java <<'EOF'
package us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze;

import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.XoroshiroRandomSource;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/**
 * Checks the segments of a column maze.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
class ColumnMazeTest {

    private static ShaftMaze column(long seed, int minY, int maxY, List<ColumnJunction> junctions) {
        List<ShaftMaze.Box> boxes = junctions.stream().map(ColumnJunction::box).toList();
        ShaftMaze.BoxLookup lookup = (min, max) -> boxes.stream().filter(box -> box.center() >= min && box.center() <= max).toList();
        return new ShaftMaze(lookup, new XoroshiroRandomSource(seed).forkPositional(), Math.floorDiv(minY, 16), Math.floorDiv(maxY - 1, 16));
    }

    private static List<ShaftMaze.Segment> segments(ShaftMaze maze) {
        List<ShaftMaze.Segment> segments = new ArrayList<>();
        int chunk = maze.minChunk();
        while (chunk <= maze.maxChunk()) {
            ShaftMaze.Segment segment = maze.segmentAt(chunk);
            segments.add(segment);
            chunk = segment.lastChunk() + 1;
        }
        return segments;
    }

    private static List<MazeEdge> edges(ShaftMaze maze) {
        List<MazeEdge> edges = new ArrayList<>();
        segments(maze).forEach(segment -> edges.addAll(segment.edges()));
        return edges;
    }

    @Test
    void columnIsOneTree() {
        for (long seed = 0; seed < 4; seed++) {
            List<ColumnJunction> junctions = List.of(new ColumnJunction(-40, 1, true), new ColumnJunction(120, -1, false));
            TreeCheck.assertOneTree(edges(column(seed, -64, 320, junctions)));
        }
    }

    @Test
    void closeJunctionsShareOneSegment() {
        List<ColumnJunction> junctions = List.of(new ColumnJunction(23, 1, true), new ColumnJunction(40, -1, false));
        ShaftMaze maze = column(3, 0, 256, junctions);
        assertTrue(segments(maze).stream().anyMatch(segment -> segment.boxes().size() == 2));
        TreeCheck.assertOneTree(edges(maze));
    }

    @Test
    void noRulingLeavesTheBuildHeight() {
        ShaftMaze maze = column(5, 0, 256, List.of(new ColumnJunction(100, 1, true)));
        for (MazeEdge edge : edges(maze)) {
            for (var node : List.of(edge.a(), edge.b())) {
                if (node instanceof MazeNode.Ruling ruling) {
                    assertTrue(ruling.along() > 0 && ruling.along() < 256, () -> "ruling at " + ruling.along());
                }
            }
        }
    }

    @Test
    void segmentsStayShort() {
        for (ShaftMaze.Segment segment : segments(column(6, -64, 320, List.of(new ColumnJunction(-40, 1, true), new ColumnJunction(200, 1, false))))) {
            assertTrue(segment.lastChunk() - segment.firstChunk() <= 2);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void oneRulingFitsBetweenJunctions() {
        assertTrue(ColumnJunction.fits(List.of(new ColumnJunction(23, 1, true), new ColumnJunction(40, 1, false))));
        assertFalse(ColumnJunction.fits(List.of(new ColumnJunction(23, 1, true), new ColumnJunction(38, 1, false))));
    }

        ShaftMaze.Box alongZ = new ColumnJunction(0, -1, false).box();
        for (int i = -2; i <= 2; i++) {

            assertEquals(2 * i, Ring.first(x));
            assertEquals(Ring.OUTER, Ring.second(x));

            assertEquals(-Ring.OUTER, Ring.first(z));
```

### Claude:

To answer your question: columns were not cut into 16-block segments. Each column had one long tree per piece, from junction to junction or from a junction to a build limit. Segmentation fixes the rings, so I made columns work like horizontal shafts. It's committed on `claude/column-maze`.

**The straight lines.** The maze sampler (Origin Shift) mixes well on a one-chunk segment but not on a 128-ruling piece. In the piece at your first spot, the lower part kept its unmixed start tree. That start tree is full rings with one vertical spine, which is what you saw.

**Columns now use segments.** I tried it in the prototype first: 200 random columns, each one tree.
- Tree and connector segments now alternate along Y, one per chunk section.
- The column patch is now part of a column junction segment, as you chose. Crossings run from the patch edge onto the nearest ring above and below.
- At the top and bottom of the world, a segment gets no link to a neighbor outside the build height.

Your seed now gives fully mixed columns, and both patches connect into the column.

**The column that ended at the junction.** That junction sat in the top slot, so its box touched the build limit. The shear band then removed the column below the box. Two of your rules now prevent this:
- The bottom and top 16-block slots hold no horizontal shafts.
- The shear band shrinks so at least 8 blocks of full column stay between the band and the outermost box.

At your second spot, the junction moved down one slot, and 8 blocks of column now stand above the box.

**Sliders.** The customize screen now has five:

| Slider | Range | Step | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| Column chance | 0 to 1 | 0.05 | 0.25 |
| Minimum shaft spacing | 32 to 1024 | 16 | 128 |
| Maximum shaft spacing | 32 to 1024 | 16 | 384 |
| Maze decay scale | 32 to 1024 | powers of two | 256 |
| Maze decay threshold | 0 to 1 | 0.05 | 0.5 |

- The spacing sliders measure center distance. If you drag one past the other, it pushes the other along.
- Shaft positions still use a grid with a random place in each cell. The cell size is the mean of the two spacings, so every gap between neighbor shafts stays between the minimum and the maximum.
- The old single spacing setting is gone.

The new spacing rule moves shafts, so your two teleport spots now show different columns.

**Checks.** All of these pass:
- the build;
- 23 JUnit tests, including a whole-column tree check and spacing limits;
- 3 GameTests;
- data generation;
- the client, which reaches the main menu with no errors;
- a dev-server world with your seed.

The world also had a trial chamber floating in the void near spawn. That comes from vanilla structures, which I haven't handled yet.

To apply the branch to `main` without a commit:

```bash
git cherry-pick -n main..claude/column-maze
```

```bash
python3 -I - "run/saves/New World (1)/level.dat" <<'EOF'
import sys, gzip, struct
b = gzip.decompress(open(sys.argv[1], 'rb').read())
def nbt(i, t):
    if t == 1: return b[i], i+1
    if t == 2: return struct.unpack('>h', b[i:i+2])[0], i+2
    if t == 3: return struct.unpack('>i', b[i:i+4])[0], i+4
    if t == 4: return struct.unpack('>q', b[i:i+8])[0], i+8
    if t == 5: return struct.unpack('>f', b[i:i+4])[0], i+4
    if t == 6: return struct.unpack('>d', b[i:i+8])[0], i+8
    if t == 7: n = struct.unpack('>i', b[i:i+4])[0]; return None, i+4+n
    if t == 8: n = struct.unpack('>H', b[i:i+2])[0]; return b[i+2:i+2+n].decode('utf8', 'replace'), i+2+n
    if t == 9:
        et = b[i]; n = struct.unpack('>i', b[i+1:i+5])[0]; i += 5; out = []
        for _ in range(n): v, i = nbt(i, et); out.append(v)
        return out, i
    if t == 10:
        d = {}
        while True:
            tt = b[i]; i += 1
            if tt == 0: return d, i
            n = struct.unpack('>H', b[i:i+2])[0]; name = b[i+2:i+2+n].decode(); i += 2+n
            d[name], i = nbt(i, tt)
    if t == 11: n = struct.unpack('>i', b[i:i+4])[0]; return None, i+4+4*n
    if t == 12: n = struct.unpack('>i', b[i:i+4])[0]; return None, i+4+8*n
root, _ = nbt(3, 10)
wgs = root['Data']['WorldGenSettings']
print('seed', wgs['seed'])
for k, v in wgs['dimensions'].items():
    g = v['generator']
    print(k, g.get('type'), g.get('settings'), g.get('aerolite'))
EOF
```

Your settings saved correctly: column chance 0.9, spacing 32 to 80, seed -777248163424996722. The cause is in placement, so I probe that shaft with these exact values.

<details>
<summary>**Tool call: `Bash`** — Probe columns along the reported shaft</summary>

```bash

package us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.AeroliteSettings;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.maze.ColumnJunction;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

class ProbeTest {
    @Test
    void probe() {
        AeroliteSettings settings = AeroliteSettings.DEFAULT.withShaftSpacing(32, 80).withColumnChance(0.9F).withDecayScale(64).withDecayThreshold(0.9F);
        ShaftLayout layout = new ShaftLayout(-777248163424996722L, "minecraft:overworld", -64, 320, settings);
        System.out.println("PROBE slots X " + java.util.Arrays.toString(layout.slots(ShaftLayout.Axis.X)) + " Z " + java.util.Arrays.toString(layout.slots(ShaftLayout.Axis.Z)));
        for (ShaftLayout.Shaft s : layout.shafts(ShaftLayout.Axis.Z, -1400, -1330)) System.out.println("PROBE Z shaft " + s);
        for (ShaftLayout.Shaft s : layout.shafts(ShaftLayout.Axis.X, -40, 40)) System.out.println("PROBE X shaft " + s);
        for (ShaftLayout.Shaft s : layout.shafts(ShaftLayout.Axis.Z, -1400, -1330)) {
            if (Math.abs(s.centerY() - 5) > 8) continue;
            int found = 0, tight = 0, total = 0;
            for (ShaftLayout.Shaft x : layout.shafts(ShaftLayout.Axis.X, -2000, 2000)) {
                total++;
                Optional<ShaftLayout.Column> c = layout.column(s.cell(), x.cell());
                if (c.isPresent()) found++;
                else if (!ColumnJunction.fits(List.of(new ColumnJunction(Math.min(s.centerY(), x.centerY()), 1, true), new ColumnJunction(Math.max(s.centerY(), x.centerY()), 1, false)))) tight++;
            }
            System.out.println("PROBE along " + s + ": crossings " + total + " columns " + found + " tight " + tight);
        }
        for (ShaftLayout.Shaft s : layout.shafts(ShaftLayout.Axis.X, -40, 40)) {
            if (Math.abs(s.centerY() - 5) > 8) continue;
            int found = 0, total = 0;
            for (ShaftLayout.Shaft z : layout.shafts(ShaftLayout.Axis.Z, -2000, 2000)) { total++; if (layout.column(z.cell(), s.cell()).isPresent()) found++; }
            System.out.println("PROBE along " + s + ": crossings " + total + " columns " + found);
        }
    }
}
EOF
```

### Claude:

<details>
<summary>**Tool call: `Write`**</summary>

File: `/Users/drullkus/IdeaProjects/aerolite/.claude/worktrees/junction-gap/src/main/java/us/drullk/aerolite/world/shaft/MixedRandomFactory.java`

```
package us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft;

import net.minecraft.util.RandomSource;
import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.PositionalRandomFactory;
import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.RandomSupport;
import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.XoroshiroRandomSource;

/**
 * A positional random factory that mixes each coordinate into the seed.
 * The vanilla Xoroshiro factory puts the coordinate hash into the seed unmixed,
 * so its first values correlate between neighbor coordinates.
 *
 * <p>Written by Claude Code; conversation transcript: {@code llm/transcripts/transcript_2026-10-29_07-05_5f965fb4-ad37-4b6a-8b5d-da8c3a179dc6.md}.
 */
public final class MixedRandomFactory implements PositionalRandomFactory {

    private final long seed;

    public MixedRandomFactory(long seed) {
        this.seed = RandomSupport.mixStafford13(seed);
    }

    /** A factory for one purpose, independent of the factories of other purposes. */
    public MixedRandomFactory fork(String name) {
        return new MixedRandomFactory(this.seed ^ name.hashCode());
    }

    /** A factory for the coordinates, independent of the randoms at other coordinates. */
    public MixedRandomFactory fork(int x, int y, int z) {
        return new MixedRandomFactory(this.mix(x, y, z));
    }

    private long mix(int x, int y, int z) {
        long hash = RandomSupport.mixStafford13(this.seed ^ x);
        hash = RandomSupport.mixStafford13(hash ^ y);
        return RandomSupport.mixStafford13(hash ^ z);
    }

    @Override
    public RandomSource at(int x, int y, int z) {
        return new XoroshiroRandomSource(this.mix(x, y, z));
    }

    @Override
    public RandomSource fromHashOf(String name) {
        return new XoroshiroRandomSource(RandomSupport.mixStafford13(this.seed ^ name.hashCode()));
    }

    @Override
    public RandomSource fromSeed(long seed) {
        return new XoroshiroRandomSource(RandomSupport.mixStafford13(this.seed ^ seed));
    }

    @Override
    public void parityConfigString(StringBuilder builder) {
        builder.append("MixedRandomFactory{").append(this.seed).append('}');
    }

}
```

### Claude:

All three issues are fixed and committed on `claude/junction-gap`. All the checks pass, but I haven't looked at the new percent labels in the client.

**Missing columns.** Your world saved its settings correctly: column chance 0.9, spacing 32 to 80. The bug was in the column roll. Every one of the 72 crossings on the shaft at x -1366 rolled above 0.9, so that shaft got no columns.
- **Cause:** the roll used the first value of Minecraft's positional random, `PositionalRandomFactory.at`. That random does not mix the coordinates into its seed, so neighboring crossings get correlated first values.
- **Fix:** every seeded shaft choice now uses a new `MixedRandomFactory`, which mixes each coordinate into the seed.
- **Test:** a new test uses your seed and settings. On every Z shaft from x -1500 to 1500, 80 to 97 percent of the allowed crossings now get a column. The old code gave 0 on your shaft.

**Close junctions.** The column at your spot had junctions at Y 23 and Y 39, with only one block between the boxes. A crossing now gets a column only when at least one slot lies between the slots of its two shafts. That leaves at least 16 blocks of full column between the two boxes. The tests check that gap on every column they find.

**Percents.** The column chance slider and the decay threshold slider now show values like 25% and 50%.

**Existing worlds.** The new random changes every layout. Existing Aerolite worlds will generate new chunks that don't line up with the old ones.

**Checks.** These pass:
- the build;
- 23 JUnit tests;
- 3 GameTests;
- data generation, which wrote no changes;
- the client, which reaches the main menu with no errors;
- a dev-server world with your seed.

CLAUDE.md now records the slot-gap rule, the percent sliders and the vanilla random behavior.

Apply the branch to `main` without a commit:

```bash
git cherry-pick -n main..claude/junction-gap
```

Columns end only at height limits in a clear pattern. Columns should terminate anywhere between the topmost junction and the top world height, and anywhere between the bottommost junction and bottom world height.

```bash
package us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft;

import net.minecraft.world.level.levelgen.XoroshiroRandomSource;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.Arrays;

class ProbeTest {
    @Test
    void probe() {
        double[] values = new double[2_000_000];
        double max = 0;
        int k = 0;
        for (long seed = 0; seed < 4; seed++) {
            FractalNoise noise = new FractalNoise(new XoroshiroRandomSource(seed), 4, 64);
            for (int i = 0; i < 500_000; i++) {
                int x = (i * 7919) % 20000, y = (i * 104729) % 384 - 64, z = (i * 1299709) % 20000;
                double v = noise.sample(x, y, z);
                values[k++] = Math.abs(v);
                max = Math.max(max, Math.abs(v));
            }
        }
        Arrays.sort(values);
        System.out.println("PROBE max " + max);
        for (double q : new double[]{0.5, 0.75, 0.9, 0.99, 0.999})
            System.out.println("PROBE |n| quantile " + q + " = " + values[(int) (q * (values.length - 1))]);
    }
}
EOF
./gradlew test --tests '*ProbeTest*' -i 2>&1 | grep PROBE; rm src/test/java/us/drullk/aerolite/world/shaft/ProbeTest.java
```

```bash
        float erosionThreshold,
        BlockState interiorBlock,""")
rep("""    public static final int MAX_DECAY_SCALE = 1024;
""","""    public static final int MAX_DECAY_SCALE = 1024;
    /** The smallest step from the decay threshold to the erosion threshold. */
    public static final float THRESHOLD_STEP = 0.05F;
""")

            Blocks.STONE.defaultBlockState(),""","""            0.5F,
            0.7F,

""","""            Codec.floatRange(0, 1).optionalFieldOf("decay_threshold", DEFAULT.decayThreshold).forGetter(AeroliteSettings::decayThreshold),
            Codec.floatRange(THRESHOLD_STEP, 1).optionalFieldOf("erosion_threshold", DEFAULT.erosionThreshold).forGetter(AeroliteSettings::erosionThreshold),
""")
rep("""    ).apply(instance, AeroliteSettings::new)).validate(settings -> settings.minShaftSpacing <= settings.maxShaftSpacing
            ? DataResult.success(settings)
            : DataResult.error(() -> "Minimum shaft spacing exceeds maximum shaft spacing: " + settings.minShaftSpacing + " > " + settings.maxShaftSpacing));""","""    ).apply(instance, AeroliteSettings::new)).validate(settings -> settings.minShaftSpacing <= settings.maxShaftSpacing
            ? DataResult.success(settings)
            : DataResult.error(() -> "Minimum shaft spacing exceeds maximum shaft spacing: " + settings.minShaftSpacing + " > " + settings.maxShaftSpacing)
    ).xmap(AeroliteSettings::withErosionAboveDecay, settings -> settings);

    /** A save from before the erosion threshold can hold a decay threshold above the default erosion threshold. */
    private AeroliteSettings withErosionAboveDecay() {
        float lowest = Math.min(1, this.decayThreshold + THRESHOLD_STEP);
        return this.erosionThreshold >= lowest ? this : this.withErosionThreshold(lowest);
```

```bash
import sys
p=sys.argv[1]; s=open(p).read()
def rep(a,b):
    global s
    assert a in s, a
    s=s.replace(a,b,1)
rep("""     * @param junctions  the two junctions in ascending sequence of Y
     */
    public record Column(Shaft xShaft, Shaft zShaft, int xShaftSign, int zShaftSign, List<ColumnJunction> junctions, int bottomShear, int topShear) {""","""     * @param junctions  the two junctions in ascending sequence of Y
     * @param bottomEnd  the lowest Y of the column
     * @param topEnd     the Y above the highest block of the column
     * @param bottomShear the height of the shear band above the bottom end
     * @param topShear   the height of the shear band below the top end
     */
    public record Column(Shaft xShaft, Shaft zShaft, int xShaftSign, int zShaftSign, List<ColumnJunction> junctions,
                         int bottomEnd, int topEnd, int bottomShear, int topShear) {

        public boolean spans(int y) {
            return y >= this.bottomEnd && y < this.topEnd;
        }""")
rep("""        int lowestBox = junctions.getFirst().center() - Ring.BOX;
        int highestBox = junctions.getLast().center() + Ring.BOX;
        int bottomShear = Math.min(MIN_SHEAR + random.nextInt(MAX_SHEAR - MIN_SHEAR + 1), Math.max(0, lowestBox - this.minY - SHEAR_MARGIN));
        int topShear = Math.min(MIN_SHEAR + random.nextInt(MAX_SHEAR - MIN_SHEAR + 1), Math.max(0, this.maxY - 1 - highestBox - SHEAR_MARGIN));
        return Optional.of(new Column(xShaft.get(), zShaft.get(), xShaftSign, zShaftSign, List.copyOf(junctions), bottomShear, topShear));""","""        // Each end leaves room for the shortest shear band and the margin to the box.
        int lowestBox = junctions.getFirst().center() - Ring.BOX;
        int highestBox = junctions.getLast().center() + Ring.BOX;
        int bottomEnd = between(random, this.minY, Math.max(this.minY, lowestBox - SHEAR_MARGIN - MIN_SHEAR));
        int topEnd = between(random, Math.min(this.maxY, highestBox + 1 + SHEAR_MARGIN + MIN_SHEAR), this.maxY);
        int bottomShear = Math.min(between(random, MIN_SHEAR, MAX_SHEAR), Math.max(0, lowestBox - SHEAR_MARGIN - bottomEnd));
        int topShear = Math.min(between(random, MIN_SHEAR, MAX_SHEAR), Math.max(0, topEnd - (highestBox + 1) - SHEAR_MARGIN));
        return Optional.of(new Column(xShaft.get(), zShaft.get(), xShaftSign, zShaftSign, List.copyOf(junctions), bottomEnd, topEnd, bottomShear, topShear));
    }

    private static int between(RandomSource random, int min, int max) {
        return min + random.nextInt(max - min + 1);""")
open(p,'w').write(s)
```

```bash
     * Decay noise beyond the erosion threshold removes blocks from the surface toward the 3x3 core.
     * The noise, normalized from the erosion threshold to 1, sets how deep the erosion reaches.
     */
    private void erode(List<ShaftLayout.Shaft> shafts, List<ShaftLayout.Column> columns) {
        for (ShaftLayout.Shaft shaft : shafts) {
            ShaftFrame frame = ShaftFrame.of(shaft);
            int alongMin = this.alongMin(shaft.axis());
            List<ShaftMaze.Box> boxes = this.layout.shaftMaze(shaft).boxesNear(alongMin, alongMin + 15);
            for (int along = alongMin; along < alongMin + 16; along++) {
                int at = along;
                if (boxes.stream().noneMatch(box -> box.covers(at))) {
                    this.erodeSection(frame, along);
                }
            }
        }
        for (ShaftLayout.Column column : columns) {
            ShaftFrame frame = ShaftFrame.of(column);
            for (int y = column.bottomEnd(); y < column.topEnd(); y++) {
                if (!inBox(column, y)) {
                    this.erodeSection(frame, y);
                }
            }
            for (ColumnJunction junction : column.junctions()) {
                this.erodeBox(column, junction);
            }
        }
    }

    /** The share of the erodible layers that erosion removes at a block, from 0 to 1. */
    private double erosionReach(BlockPos pos) {
        double value = Math.abs(this.layout.decayNoise().sample(pos.getX(), pos.getY(), pos.getZ()));
        double threshold = this.settings.erosionThreshold();
        return value <= threshold ? 0 : (value - threshold) / (1 - threshold);
    }

    private void erodeSection(ShaftFrame frame, int along) {
        int layers = Ring.OUTER - 1;
        for (int first = -Ring.OUTER; first <= Ring.OUTER; first++) {
            for (int second = -Ring.OUTER; second <= Ring.OUTER; second++) {
                int depth = Ring.OUTER - Math.max(Math.abs(first), Math.abs(second));
                BlockPos pos = frame.at(along, first, second);
                if (depth < layers && this.inChunk(pos) && depth < this.erosionReach(pos) * layers) {
                    this.chunk.setBlockState(pos, AIR, false);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private void erodeBox(ShaftLayout.Column column, ColumnJunction junction) {
        int layers = Ring.BOX - 1;
        for (int dx = -Ring.BOX; dx <= Ring.BOX; dx++) {
            for (int dy = -Ring.BOX; dy <= Ring.BOX; dy++) {
```

```bash
p=sys.argv[1]; s=open(p).read()
a=s.index("        contents.addChild(new StepSlider(FRACTION_STEPS, Math.round(this.settings.decayThreshold()")
b=s.index("\n\n", a)

        StepSlider[] thresholds = new StepSlider[2];
        thresholds[0] = new StepSlider(FRACTION_STEPS, Math.round(this.settings.decayThreshold() * FRACTION_STEPS),
                step -> Component.translatable("createWorld.customize.aerolite.decay_threshold", percent(step)),

                    int decay = Math.min(step, FRACTION_STEPS - 1);
                    int erosion = Math.max(decay + 1, Math.round(this.settings.erosionThreshold() * FRACTION_STEPS));
                    this.settings = this.settings.withDecayThreshold(decay / (float) FRACTION_STEPS).withErosionThreshold(erosion / (float) FRACTION_STEPS);
                    thresholds[0].setStep(decay);
                    thresholds[1].setStep(erosion - 1);
```

```bash
package us.drullk.aerolite.gametest;

import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries;
import net.minecraft.gametest.framework.GameTest;
import net.minecraft.gametest.framework.GameTestHelper;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.level.ChunkPos;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.ProtoChunk;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.UpgradeData;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.gametest.GameTestHolder;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.gametest.PrefixGameTestTemplate;
import us.drullk.aerolite.Aerolite;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.AeroliteSettings;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.ShaftLayout;
import us.drullk.aerolite.world.shaft.ShaftPainter;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

@GameTestHolder(Aerolite.MODID)
@PrefixGameTestTemplate(false)
public final class ErosionDumpGameTests {
    @GameTest(template = "empty")
    public static void dumpErosion(GameTestHelper helper) throws IOException {
        ServerLevel level = helper.getLevel();
        AeroliteSettings settings = AeroliteSettings.DEFAULT.withDecayThreshold(0.25F).withErosionThreshold(0.35F).withDecayScale(64);
        ShaftLayout layout = new ShaftLayout(-777248163424996722L, "minecraft:overworld", -64, 320, settings);
        ShaftLayout.Column column = null;
        for (int i = 0; i < 40 && column == null; i++) for (int j = 0; j < 40 && column == null; j++) {
            Optional<ShaftLayout.Column> c = layout.column(i, j);
            if (c.isPresent()) column = c.get();
        }
        List<String> lines = new ArrayList<>();
        lines.add("column " + column);
        int reach = 30;
        for (int cx = (column.x() - reach) >> 4; cx <= (column.x() + reach) >> 4; cx++) for (int cz = (column.z() - reach) >> 4; cz <= (column.z() + reach) >> 4; cz++) {
            ChunkPos pos = new ChunkPos(cx, cz);
            ProtoChunk chunk = new ProtoChunk(pos, UpgradeData.EMPTY, level, level.registryAccess().registryOrThrow(Registries.BIOME), null);
            ShaftPainter.paint(layout, chunk);
            for (int x = pos.getMinBlockX(); x <= pos.getMaxBlockX(); x++) for (int z = pos.getMinBlockZ(); z <= pos.getMaxBlockZ(); z++) {
                if (Math.abs(x - column.x()) > reach || Math.abs(z - column.z()) > reach) continue;
                for (int y = -64; y < 320; y++) {
                    BlockState b = chunk.getBlockState(new BlockPos(x, y, z));
                    int code = b.isAir() ? 0 : b == settings.interiorBlock() ? 1 : b == settings.wallBlock() ? 2 : b == settings.pathBlock() ? 3 : 9;
                    if (code != 0) lines.add((x - column.x()) + " " + y + " " + (z - column.z()) + " " + code);
                }
            }
        }
        Files.write(Path.of("erosion_dump.txt"), lines);
        helper.succeed();
    }
}
EOF
```

### Claude:

Shaft erosion and random column ends are both in, committed on `claude/erosion`. The checks pass, but I haven't clicked through the new slider in the client.

**Erosion**
- **Where it starts:** where the absolute decay noise passes the erosion threshold, at either end of the noise.
- **How deep it goes:** depth = (|noise| − erosion threshold) / (1.0 − erosion threshold). That value sets what share of the removable layers goes at each block.
- **What never vanishes:**
  - A shaft has 5 removable layers, and its 3x3 core always stays.
  - A junction box has 6 removable layers, measured from the box face, and its 3x3x3 core always stays.
- **How it looks:** the noise is read at each block, so one side of a shaft can erode deeper than the other. Cross sections in a test render show exactly that, with the core intact.
- **Erosion slider:** 5% to 100% in steps of 5%, default 70%.
  - It pushes the decay threshold slider to stay at least 5% below it, so the decay threshold now tops out at 95%.
  - The measured noise rarely passes 0.72 (only 0.1 percent of blocks), so the 70% default erodes very little. Full depth would need noise near 1.0, which never occurs.
- **Old saves:** your world with a 90% decay threshold still loads. Its erosion threshold is raised to 95%.

**Column ends**
- **Top end:** uniform from 16 blocks above the highest junction box to the top build limit.
- **Bottom end:** uniform from the bottom build limit to 16 blocks below the lowest junction box.
- **Why 16:** that space holds the shortest shear band plus the 8-block margin, so a column ending as close as possible to its junction still gets a ragged end.
- **Shear band:** each end picks 8 to 64 blocks, then shrinks so 8 blocks of full column stay between the band and the box.
- **Maze paths:** the column now leaves no paths in the air past its ends.

**Checks**
- **Passing:**
  - the build;
  - 24 JUnit tests, including column end ranges and old-save repair;
  - 4 GameTests, including one where strong erosion leaves the 3x3 core whole;
  - data generation;
  - the client, which reaches the main menu with no errors;
  - a dev-server world with your seed.
- **One failure:** the first build run failed in the Beanification transform task (`beanificationTransformClassesIdea`). The same command passed on the next run with no change, and I don't know the cause.

CLAUDE.md now has the erosion and column end rules, plus the Erosion term in the glossary.

To apply the branch to `main` without a commit:

```bash
git cherry-pick -n main..claude/erosion
```

### User:

Merged. A fix:

At `/execute in minecraft:overworld run tp @s -65.97 265.44 -408.19 -624.60 41.40` the junction box erodes to the point of disconnecting both shafts passing through it. Shaft cores should persist through junction decay.

----

### Claude:

The cause: `erodeBox` keeps only the box's own 3x3x3 center. The 3x3 cores of the shafts that pass through the box get no protection there, so deep erosion cuts them. The fix keeps every box block on the 3x3 core line of the horizontal shaft and of the column.

```bash
    /** True on the 3x3 core line of the column or of the horizontal shaft through the box. */
    private static boolean onShaftCore(ColumnJunction junction, int dx, int dy, int dz) {
        boolean column = Math.abs(dx) <= 1 && Math.abs(dz) <= 1;
        boolean horizontal = junction.alongX()
                ? Math.abs(dy) <= 1 && Math.abs(dz) <= 1
                : Math.abs(dy) <= 1 && Math.abs(dx) <= 1;
        return column || horizontal;
    }
```

With:

```
    @GameTest(template = "empty")
    public static void erosionKeepsTheShaftCoresThroughJunctionBoxes(GameTestHelper helper) {
        ServerLevel level = helper.getLevel();
        AeroliteSettings settings = AeroliteSettings.DEFAULT.withDecayThreshold(0).withErosionThreshold(AeroliteSettings.THRESHOLD_STEP).withDecayScale(32);
        ShaftLayout layout = new ShaftLayout(42, "test", level.getMinBuildHeight(), level.getMaxBuildHeight(), settings);
        ShaftLayout.Column column = null;
        for (int cell = 0; column == null; cell++) {
            column = layout.column(cell, cell).or(() -> Optional.empty()).orElse(null);
            for (int other = 0; column == null && other < 40; other++) {
                column = layout.column(cell, other).orElse(null);
            }
        }
        Map<Long, ProtoChunk> chunks = new HashMap<>();
        int boxAir = 0;
        for (ColumnJunction junction : column.junctions()) {
            for (int a = -Ring.BOX; a <= Ring.BOX; a++) {
                for (int u = -1; u <= 1; u++) {
                    for (int v = -1; v <= 1; v++) {
                        BlockPos columnCore = new BlockPos(column.x() + u, junction.center() + a, column.z() + v);
                        BlockPos shaftCore = junction.alongX()
                                ? new BlockPos(column.x() + a, junction.center() + u, column.z() + v)
                                : new BlockPos(column.x() + v, junction.center() + u, column.z() + a);
                        helper.assertFalse(paintedAt(chunks, layout, level, columnCore).isAir(), "Erosion cut the column core in a junction box");
                        helper.assertFalse(paintedAt(chunks, layout, level, shaftCore).isAir(), "Erosion cut the shaft core in a junction box");
                    }
                }
            }
            for (int d = -Ring.BOX; d <= Ring.BOX; d++) {
                if (paintedAt(chunks, layout, level, new BlockPos(column.x() + Ring.BOX, junction.center() + d, column.z() + Ring.BOX)).isAir()) {
                    boxAir++;
                }
            }
        }
        helper.assertTrue(boxAir > 0, "Erosion removed no box edge block");
        helper.succeed();
    }

    private static BlockState paintedAt(Map<Long, ProtoChunk> chunks, ShaftLayout layout, ServerLevel level, BlockPos pos) {
        ChunkPos chunkPos = new ChunkPos(pos);
        ProtoChunk chunk = chunks.computeIfAbsent(chunkPos.toLong(), key -> {
            ProtoChunk painted = new ProtoChunk(chunkPos, UpgradeData.EMPTY, level, level.registryAccess().registryOrThrow(Registries.BIOME), null);
            ShaftPainter.paint(layout, painted);
            return painted;
        });
        return chunk.getBlockState(pos);
    }
```

### Claude:

Fixed and committed on `claude/box-cores`. Erosion can no longer cut a shaft at a junction. I didn't look at your exact spot in game.

**Cause:** box erosion only protected the box's own 3x3x3 center. Outside that center, the cores of both shafts were open to erosion inside the box. Deep erosion cut them there and disconnected the shafts.

**Fix:** box erosion now also keeps every block on the 3x3 core line of the column and of the horizontal shaft, all the way through the box. The shaft cores already survived outside the box, so the cores now run unbroken through every junction.

**Test:** a new GameTest erodes a real column with an erosion threshold of 5%. It checks every core block through both of its boxes. It also confirms the box itself still erodes, so the fix doesn't simply turn erosion off. Against the old code it fails with "Erosion cut the column core in a junction box".

**Checks:** the build, JUnit, 5 GameTests and data generation pass. The client reaches the main menu, and a dev-server world with your seed generates and saves without errors.

CLAUDE.md now states the core-line rule.

To apply the branch to `main` without a commit:

```bash
git cherry-pick -n main..claude/box-cores
```

```bash
import sys, gzip, struct
b = gzip.decompress(open(sys.argv[1], 'rb').read())
def nbt(i, t):
    if t == 1: return b[i], i+1
    if t == 2: return struct.unpack('>h', b[i:i+2])[0], i+2
    if t == 3: return struct.unpack('>i', b[i:i+4])[0], i+4
    if t == 4: return struct.unpack('>q', b[i:i+8])[0], i+8
    if t == 5: return struct.unpack('>f', b[i:i+4])[0], i+4
    if t == 6: return struct.unpack('>d', b[i:i+8])[0], i+8
    if t == 7: n = struct.unpack('>i', b[i:i+4])[0]; return None, i+4+n
    if t == 8: n = struct.unpack('>H', b[i:i+2])[0]; return b[i+2:i+2+n].decode('utf8', 'replace'), i+2+n
    if t == 9:
        et = b[i]; n = struct.unpack('>i', b[i+1:i+5])[0]; i += 5; out = []
        for _ in range(n): v, i = nbt(i, et); out.append(v)
        return out, i
    if t == 10:
        d = {}
        while True:
            tt = b[i]; i += 1
            if tt == 0: return d, i
            n = struct.unpack('>H', b[i:i+2])[0]; name = b[i+2:i+2+n].decode(); i += 2+n
            d[name], i = nbt(i, tt)
    if t == 11: n = struct.unpack('>i', b[i:i+4])[0]; return None, i+4+4*n
    if t == 12: n = struct.unpack('>i', b[i:i+4])[0]; return None, i+4+8*n
root, _ = nbt(3, 10)
wgs = root['Data']['WorldGenSettings']
print('seed', wgs['seed'], root['Data'].get('LevelName'))
print(wgs['dimensions']['minecraft:overworld']['generator'].get('aerolite'))
EOF
```

```bash
python3 - src/main/java/us/drullk/aerolite/world/shaft/ShaftPainter.java <<'EOF'
import sys
p=sys.argv[1]; s=open(p).read()
a=s.index("    private void erodeBox(ShaftLayout.Column column, ColumnJunction junction) {")
b=s.index("    /** Near each column end, a threshold that rises toward the end removes column blocks. */")
s=s[:a]+'''    private void erodeBox(ShaftLayout.Column column, ColumnJunction junction) {
        for (int dx = -Ring.BOX; dx <= Ring.BOX; dx++) {
            for (int dy = -Ring.BOX; dy <= Ring.BOX; dy++) {
                for (int dz = -Ring.BOX; dz <= Ring.BOX; dz++) {
                    BlockPos pos = new BlockPos(column.x() + dx, junction.center() + dy, column.z() + dz);
                    if (this.inChunk(pos) && this.erosionReach(pos) > boxResistance(junction, dx, dy, dz)) {
                        this.chunk.setBlockState(pos, AIR, false);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * The erosion resistance of a box block, from 0 at a surface to 1 at a core.
     * It is the largest of the box resistance and the resistance of each shaft through the block.
     */
    private static double boxResistance(ColumnJunction junction, int dx, int dy, int dz) {
        double box = (double) (Ring.BOX - Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.max(Math.abs(dy), Math.abs(dz)))) / (Ring.BOX - 1);
        double column = sectionResistance(dx, dz);
        double horizontal = junction.alongX() ? sectionResistance(dy, dz) : sectionResistance(dy, dx);
        return Math.max(box, Math.max(column, horizontal));
    }

    /** The erosion resistance in a shaft cross section, 0 outside the section and 1 at the 3x3 core. */
    private static double sectionResistance(int first, int second) {
        int offset = Math.max(Math.abs(first), Math.abs(second));
        return offset > Ring.OUTER ? 0 : Math.min(1, (double) (Ring.OUTER - offset) / (Ring.OUTER - 1));
    }

'''+s[b:]
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
./gradlew compileJava 2>&1 | grep -E "error|BUILD"
```

### Claude:

Fixed, using the maximum resistance as you described. It's committed on `claude/box-resistance`.

**Why it still cut:** your `main` is still at the erosion commit. The earlier core-line fix on `claude/box-cores` was never applied there, so your world ran without it. I replayed your spot with your build's painter, your seed and your settings. At column x 160, z 1370, the junction at Y 168 lost 3 of the 9 shaft core blocks at the box face, where the shaft enters.

**Change:** each block now has an erosion resistance from 0 at a surface to 1 at a core.
- A box block takes the largest of the box's own resistance and the resistance of each shaft whose cross section holds it.
- Erosion removes a block only when the erosion depth exceeds that resistance.
- So a box never erodes deeper into a shaft than the shaft itself does, and both shaft cores keep a resistance of 1 through the box.
- Shaft sections now use the same function, so the rule is identical inside and outside boxes.

**Result at your spot:** every core block of the shaft and the column survives through the box. Inside the box, the shaft's cross section keeps 165 to 169 of its 169 blocks.

**Checks:** the build, JUnit, 5 GameTests and data generation pass. The client reaches the main menu, and a dev-server world with your seed generates and saves without errors. The GameTest that erodes a real junction fails on your current painter and passes on this one.

CLAUDE.md now describes erosion in terms of resistance.

This branch builds on `claude/box-cores`, so one command applies both fixes:

```bash
git cherry-pick -n main..claude/box-resistance
```
